**Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip pass.
- **Field count:** 14 declared (11 writable + 3 display-only that still persist when the React form posts them) + `dashboard_menu_manager` leak + `unknown_printful_field` probe ⇒ 16 keys in DB after both saves.
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE, unfiltered.** Save B's payload completely replaced Save A's payload (every Save A key whose Save B counterpart was `''` reached DB as `''`). Recurring Tasks 8-17 pattern.
- **Empty-option default:** `get_option('dokan_printful', [])` → `[]` when the DB row is absent. Schema defaults NEVER injected on read. Captured to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_printful_empty_default.json`.
- **Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak:** present in DB after both saves (Task 12 pattern, unchanged).
- **Unknown-key persistence:** Save B included `unknown_printful_field='__T_FAKE_UNKNOWN_001'` (not declared anywhere in schema). Persisted to DB and round-tripped through `dokan_get_setting_values` unchanged. Same as Tasks 9, 11, 13, 15, 17.
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **Yes** — `printful_secret_key` returns `"[BLOCKED: Sensitive key]"` in both the post-save response and the GET endpoint, while the DB stores the literal value. Same wrapper mechanism as Task 17 (`auth_token`). **Important distinction:** `printful_client_id` (which Printful treats as paired-with-secret OAuth credential material) is **NOT** masked — it round-trips verbatim in the response. The masking logic appears to key on field name string match only.
- **Schema vs read-site key skew — CATASTROPHIC (worst observed so far, worse than Tasks 16 & 17):** Of the 11 writable schema fields, only **1 shares a name with a read site** (`size_guide_button_text`). The other **10 are dead** at every read site:
  - `printful_enable` → no read site reads this (enable is implicit via app_id/app_secret presence).
  - `printful_client_id` → read sites use `app_id`.
  - `printful_secret_key` → read sites use `app_secret`.
  - `size_guide_popup_title` → read site uses `popup_title`.
  - `size_guide_popup_text_color` → read site uses `popup_text_color`.
  - `size_guide_popup_background_color` → read site uses `popup_bg_color`.
  - `size_guide_tab_background_color` → read site uses `tab_bg_color`.
  - `size_guide_active_tab_background_color` → read site uses `active_tab_bg_color`.
  - `size_guide_button_text_color` → read site uses `button_text_color`.
  - `size_guide_measurement_unit` → read site uses `primary_measurement_unit` (plus value-spelling drift `centimeter` vs legacy `centimetre`).
  In addition, **3 display-only fields persist as dead strings** (`printful_app_name`, `printful_app_url`, `printful_redirection_domains`) — the legacy `Settings.php:117-132` derives these dynamically from `home_url()` and `wp_parse_url()`; the new schema persists whatever the React form posts and never derives them. **Bottom line: 13 of 14 schema fields are dead.** Without one of (a) rename schema keys to match read sites, (b) rename read sites to match schema, or (c) save-side mirror, **the Printful integration is non-functional from the new settings page** (admin cannot connect their Printful app at all, since `app_id`/`app_secret` cannot be set anywhere). Same class of defect as Task 17 but on a tab where the user-visible failure is total (OAuth never completes).
- **No on-save validation:** invalid enum (`'furlongs'` for `size_guide_measurement_unit`), invalid color (`'notacolor'`), XSS string in `size_guide_button_text`, empty credential fields, unknown key — all persisted verbatim. Same generic `sanitize_text_field` walk as every other tab. Particularly notable here because the legacy schema declares typed callbacks (`type: 'color'`, `type: 'select'` with bounded `options`) that the new flat-array schema discards.
- **Dependency gating is display-only:** UI-side dependencies (`printful_enable==='on'` gates visibility of the API-settings group) gate visibility only; the AJAX save endpoint accepts every child key regardless of parent. Save B with `printful_enable='off'` still stored all `printful_*` and `size_guide_*` keys (Tasks 10, 13, 15-17 pattern).
- **Display-only fields persisting:** Three schema fields with `variant: 'info'` / `variant: 'copy_field'` (`printful_app_name`, `printful_app_url`, `printful_redirection_domains`) are declared as non-input UI elements but the React form posts them and the server stores them. The legacy Pro module derived URL/domain dynamically server-side at render time (`Settings.php:117-132` builds `$redirect_url = admin_url(...)` and `$domain = wp_parse_url(home_url())['host']`). The new path will need to either suppress these in the React payload or have the server re-derive them on read.
- **Round-trip:** 14 declared fields + 1 unknown-key probe (`unknown_printful_field`) + Pro MenuManager leak (`dashboard_menu_manager: []`) — all round-trip correctly through AJAX `dokan_get_setting_values` (`_trace_artifacts/dokan_printful_reload.json`), modulo the `printful_secret_key` safety-wrapper masking. DB state in `_trace_artifacts/dokan_printful_after_b.json` is byte-identical to the values posted in Save B.
- **API-key/token state in DB after task:** **CLEARED.** Final step ran `wp option delete dokan_printful` so no sentinel strings remain in DB (`wp option get dokan_printful` returns `Error: option does not exist`). All sentinels in artifacts are synthetic `__T_FAKE_KEY_PRINTFUL_001*` placeholders — no real Printful OAuth client IDs or secrets. `grep -E 'AC[a-f0-9]{32}|sk_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|Bearer\s+[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}' _trace_artifacts/dokan_printful_*.json` returned empty (exit 1). Per-vendor OAuth tokens in `user_meta` (`dokan_printful_access_token`, `_refresh_token`, `_expired_at`, `_store_id`) are out of scope for this trace and untouched.
- **Surprises (NEW vs Tasks 1-17):**
  - **Sensitive wrapper covers only one of a credential pair.** `printful_secret_key` is masked in responses but its paired `printful_client_id` is not — the wrapper's key list is not pair-aware. New finding vs Task 17 (where `auth_token` was masked but `account_sid` was not, also single-key — but here the unmasked half is itself credential material that, combined with a leaked client ID, weakens OAuth integration security).
  - **Total credential capture is impossible via schema.** Unlike Task 17 where the SMS gateway tab had partial key skew (3/11 matched), Printful's tab has the entire OAuth credential pair (`app_id`/`app_secret`) reachable in schema only under different names (`printful_client_id`/`printful_secret_key`). The new settings page cannot configure a working Printful integration AT ALL.
  - **Display-only fields persist as zombies.** Three fields the legacy code derived server-side at render time are now persisted as user-supplied strings (NEW vs all prior tabs — earlier traces did not exercise `variant: 'info'` / `variant: 'copy_field'` write paths because those fields didn't exist in Lite tabs).
  - **No defaults injection still applies.** Even though schema declares 7 colour/text defaults plus `size_guide_measurement_unit='inches'`, the empty option returns `[]`. Same as every prior Pro tab — confirming the Pro modules do not register a `sanitize_options('read')` callback that mixes defaults in.
- **Commit SHA:** `5fb76bf26` (amended in-place).
- **Concerns:**
  - **P0 — Integration non-functional from new settings UI:** `printful_client_id`/`printful_secret_key` schema keys do not feed `Auth.php`'s OAuth client. Either rename schema keys to `app_id`/`app_secret` (preferred — fewest moving parts and keeps existing per-vendor tokens valid) or add a save-side mirror.
  - **P1 — Display-only fields persist:** `printful_app_name`, `printful_app_url`, `printful_redirection_domains` are derived in legacy code. Have the React form omit them from the save payload, or have the schema declare them server-derived on read.
  - **P1 — Sensitive-key wrapper coverage gap:** confirm the wrapper's key list during plugin-ui migration so it masks both halves of credential pairs (`printful_secret_key`+`printful_client_id`, `account_sid`+`auth_token` from Task 17).
  - **P2 — Value-format drift:** `size_guide_measurement_unit` enum value `'centimeter'` vs legacy `'centimetre'` — if read sites are updated to the new key, also update the value comparison or sites will treat the new value as falsey.
  - **P2 — `printful_enable` toggle without effect:** toggling off does not revoke vendor tokens or remove the integration; either wire it in or remove from the schema to avoid admin confusion.

### `dokan_product_subscription` — Vendor Subscription (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_product_subscription`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_product_subscription` returned 11 keys: `subscription_pack='61'`, `enable_pricing='on'`, `enable_subscription_pack_in_reg='on'`, `notify_by_email=''`, `no_of_days_before_mail='2'`, `product_status_after_end='draft'`, `cancelling_email_subject='Subscription Package Cancel notification'`, `cancelling_email_body=…`, `alert_email_subject='Subscription Ending Soon'`, `alert_email_body=…`, `dashboard_menu_manager=[]`. After `wp option delete dokan_product_subscription` the AJAX GET returned `[]` (schema defaults NOT injected on read) — captured to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_product_subscription_empty_default.json`.

**Source path:**
- Module bootstrap: `dokan-pro/modules/subscription/module.php` (one-shot at activation sets `subscription_pack` to a newly inserted auto-draft page — module.php:240-253 — only if the option does not already carry a pack id).
- **Legacy schema:** `dokan-pro/modules/subscription/includes/admin/admin.php:870-922` `add_new_setting_field_admin_panael` filters `dokan_settings_fields` p12. Declares **5 fields** for the AJAX settings UI: `subscription_pack` (select of pages), `enable_pricing` (switcher), `enable_subscription_pack_in_reg` (switcher, default `on`), `no_of_days_before_mail` (text, default `'2'`), `product_status_after_end` (select; options `publish|pending|draft`). NB: legacy schema does **not** declare `notify_by_email`, `cancelling_email_*`, `alert_email_*` — but those keys are present in DB (seeded by older versions / runtime defaults) and are read by the Pro runtime.
- **Current-branch schema (plugin-ui flat array):** `dokan-pro/modules/subscription/includes/classes/Schema/SubscriptionSettingsSchema.php`. Filter `dokan_get_admin_settings_schema` p20 appends one subpage `vendor_subscription` (priority 400, page_id `vendor`) plus section `vendor_subscription_section`. Declares **10 fields**: `vendor_subscription` (switch, default `on`), `subscription_view_page` (select), `subscription_in_registration` (switch, default `off`), `email_notification_expiry` (switch, default `on`), `alert_days_before_expiry` (number, default `10`), `products_status_on_expiry` (radio_capsule, default `'draft'`, options `draft|published|pending_review`), `cancelling_email_subject` (textarea), `cancelling_email_body` (textarea), `alert_email_subject` (textarea), `alert_email_body` (textarea).
- **Read sites (Pro runtime):**
  - `module.php:58` → `enable_pricing` (gates entire module bootstrap; key in old name).
  - `module.php:234, 272, 449, 568, 1304, 2655` and `Helper.php` → `subscription_pack` (page id used everywhere — vendor subscription page link, shortcode injection target, redirects).
  - `module.php:916` → `cancelling_email_subject`.
  - `module.php:917` → `cancelling_email_body`.
  - `module.php:1958` → `enable_subscription_pack_in_reg`.
  - `module.php:2545` → `enable_pricing` again (vendor capability gate).
  - `includes/emails/subscription-expiry-alert-vendor.php:66` → `alert_email_subject`.
  - `includes/classes/HelperChangerProductStatus.php:32` → `product_status_after_end` (cron-driven status flip when packs expire).
  - `includes/classes/Helper.php:57` → `enable_pricing`.
  - `includes/classes/Shortcode.php:82-97` → `subscription_pack` (consumed inside the `dokan_after_saving_settings` hook to overwrite the chosen page's `post_content` with `[dps_product_pack]`).
- **Save handler:** `dokan-lite/includes/Admin/Settings.php:150 save_settings_value()`. Nonce `dokan_admin`, action `dokan_save_settings`, OVERWRITE via `update_option('dokan_product_subscription', $value)`. No subscription-module-specific save filter (no `dokan_save_settings_value` handler from this module).
- **Filters/actions touching this option:**
  - generic `dokan_save_settings_value` filter (Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager:[]`);
  - **`dokan_after_saving_settings` action (subscription module, Shortcode.php:32)** — this fires after every save of `dokan_product_subscription` and rewrites the `subscription_pack` page's `post_content` to literally `[dps_product_pack]` via `wp_update_post`. **Side-effect on every save.** No corresponding "before" handler.
- **Side effects on save:** (a) `wp_update_post` on the page referenced by `subscription_pack` → its `post_content` is hard-overwritten to `[dps_product_pack]`. Save A (subscription_pack=`'61'`) WILL have re-blasted that page's content during this trace; Save B (subscription_pack=`''`) skips the rewrite because of the early-return guard (`if ( ! $page_id ) return;`). No cron schedule/unschedule on save (the `dps_schedule_pack_update` daily cron is scheduled only at module activation/deactivation — module.php:227, 322 — not from this option). No transient flush, no rewrite-rules touch.

**Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip pass.
- **Field count:** 11 keys in initial DB → 18 keys in DB after Save A (11 + 6 new-schema probes + 1 unknown probe + the MenuManager leak, which was already there) → 17 keys in DB after Save B (probe removed; OVERWRITE confirmed).
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE, unfiltered.** Save B's payload completely replaced Save A's payload — `unknown_product_subscription_field` (present in A, omitted in B) is GONE from `_trace_artifacts/dokan_product_subscription_after_b.json`. All Save B values (including invalid enums `'invalid_status'`, `'bogusvalue'`, non-numeric `'notanumber'`, XSS string, empty strings) persisted byte-identical. Recurring Tasks 8-18 pattern.
- **Empty-option default:** `get_option('dokan_product_subscription', [])` → `[]` after `wp option delete dokan_product_subscription`. **Schema defaults NEVER injected on read** (recurring Pro pattern). Captured to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_product_subscription_empty_default.json`.
- **Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak:** present in DB after both saves (Task 12 pattern, unchanged).
- **Unknown-key persistence:** Save A's `unknown_product_subscription_field='__T_FAKE_UNKNOWN_A'` persisted; Save B (omitted it) dropped it via OVERWRITE.
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** No fields on this tab are masked in either the GET endpoint or the post-save response. No values on this tab match the sensitive-key list from Tasks 17 (`auth_token`) / 18 (`printful_secret_key`). Email subject/body are stored verbatim (XSS probe round-trips).
- **Schema↔read-site key skew audit:**
  - **Legacy schema (5 declared fields) vs Pro read sites:** matched **5/5** (`subscription_pack`, `enable_pricing`, `enable_subscription_pack_in_reg`, `no_of_days_before_mail`, `product_status_after_end` are all read at module.php / Helper.php / HelperChangerProductStatus.php).
  - **New plugin-ui schema (10 declared fields) vs Pro read sites:** matched **4/10** — only `cancelling_email_subject`, `cancelling_email_body`, `alert_email_subject`, `alert_email_body` are read at runtime under those exact names. The other **6/10 are renamed and dead**: `vendor_subscription` (read site uses `enable_pricing`), `subscription_view_page` (uses `subscription_pack`), `subscription_in_registration` (uses `enable_subscription_pack_in_reg`), `email_notification_expiry` (no read site; closest legacy peer `notify_by_email` also dead), `alert_days_before_expiry` (uses `no_of_days_before_mail`), `products_status_on_expiry` (uses `product_status_after_end`, AND new schema values `published`/`pending_review` don't match the WP post_status whitelist `publish`/`pending`).
  - **DB-only keys (in initial GET, declared by neither schema):** `notify_by_email` (1 — ghost; read by no one), `dashboard_menu_manager` (1 — Pro MenuManager leak), `alert_email_body` (declared by NEW schema but read by no Pro code — present only because email template uses i18n default text). Effective dead-key fraction in new schema: **60% (6/10) — comparable to Tasks 16-18 (73-91%) but improved here because the email subject/body keys were kept identical**.
- **Side effect on save — page rewrite:** `dokan_after_saving_settings → Shortcode::insert_shortcode_into_page` (Shortcode.php:81-102) overwrites the `subscription_pack` page's `post_content` to `[dps_product_pack]` on every save where `subscription_pack` is non-empty. **NEW vs every prior tab:** this is the first traced tab where a save action mutates `wp_posts`. After Save A in this trace, page #61's post_content was reset to `[dps_product_pack]` (the original install content). Save B with `subscription_pack=''` skipped the rewrite. If the new schema's renamed key `subscription_view_page` is wired in instead, the Pro hook handler (which only looks at `$value['subscription_pack']`) will silently no-op — the dashboard page will no longer be auto-content-managed.
- **Module-activation default:** `module.php:240-253` on module activation inserts an auto-draft page and writes its post id into `dokan_product_subscription.subscription_pack`. This is the only path that ever populates `subscription_pack` automatically — manual UI selection is the other path. After Task 19 the option already had `subscription_pack='61'` from a prior install; this trace did NOT trigger the activation path.
- **Cron `dps_schedule_pack_update`:** scheduled in `module.php:227` at module activation, cleared in `module.php:322` at deactivation. **NOT touched by save**. Cron daily handler reads `product_status_after_end` to flip expired vendors' products — under the new schema name `products_status_on_expiry` the cron will fall through to its default `'draft'`.
- **No on-save validation:** invalid enums (`'invalid_status'`, `'bogusvalue'`), non-numeric in numeric field (`'notanumber'`), XSS string in subject, empty required fields — all persisted verbatim via `sanitize_options($value, 'edit')`'s generic `sanitize_text_field` walk. Same pattern as every prior tab.
- **Round-trip:** all 17 keys (legacy 11 - unknown_probe + 6 new-schema probes) round-trip correctly through AJAX `dokan_get_setting_values` — `_trace_artifacts/dokan_product_subscription_reload.json` is byte-identical to the Save B response value and to the on-disk DB row (`_trace_artifacts/dokan_product_subscription_after_b.json` after key-order normalization).
- **DB state restored:** after capturing artifacts, ran `wp option update dokan_product_subscription <orig>` from `/tmp/dokan_product_subscription_orig.json`. Page #61's `post_content` was reset to `[dps_product_pack]` by Save A and was NOT restored (the original page content is not in this trace's scope — but note this is a live side effect of the save handler).
- **Surprises (NEW vs Tasks 1-18):**
  - **Save mutates `wp_posts`.** First traced tab where `dokan_after_saving_settings` causes a `wp_update_post` write. Tasks 1-18 only ever touched `wp_options`. The subscription module silently rewrites the chosen page's content to `[dps_product_pack]` every time admin saves. This is destructive of any custom page content that admin/customer might have edited.
  - **Ghost field in DB.** `notify_by_email` is present in the initial DB row, accepted by the save endpoint, and read by **zero** Pro code — declared by neither legacy nor new schema. Likely seeded by a long-removed migration. Tasks 1-18 had unknown probe keys we injected; this is the first tab where the BASELINE option already carries an undeclared key.
  - **Value-format drift on a status enum.** `products_status_on_expiry` declares values (`published`, `pending_review`) that do NOT match WP core `post_status` strings (`publish`, `pending`). If the read site (`HelperChangerProductStatus.php:32`) is ever migrated to the new key, the cron-driven status flip will fail silently via `wp_update_post`. NEW finding — prior tabs had key-name skew but not value-format skew on a `post_status` enum.
  - **Schema field that no read site has ever read.** `alert_email_body` is declared by the new schema, was already in DB before this trace (seeded by older install), but no Pro PHP code references it. The email template uses a hard-coded i18n default. NEW: prior tabs had dead schema keys due to renames, this one is dead by historical decay.
  - **Legacy schema declares FEWER fields than DB carries.** Legacy `add_new_setting_field_admin_panael` returns 5 keys; DB carries 10 functional keys + the menu-manager leak. Tasks 1-18 showed schema as a superset of DB; this tab inverts it — half the persisted state is invisible in the legacy admin UI. The new plugin-ui schema (10 declared) finally surfaces them.
- **API-key/token state in DB after task:** **None present** — this tab has no credential material. `grep -E 'AC[a-f0-9]{32}|sk_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|Bearer\s+[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}' _trace_artifacts/dokan_product_subscription_*.json` returned empty.
- **Commit SHA:** `916d5b090`.
- **Concerns:**
  - **P0 — Save-time `wp_update_post` is destructive.** Every save with non-empty `subscription_pack` rewrites the target page's `post_content` to literally `[dps_product_pack]`. Any customizations to that page (theme blocks, vendor onboarding copy, etc.) are wiped. Plugin-ui migration should either (a) keep the same legacy key name so the existing handler still finds it (preferred — preserves the unconditional rewrite is bad UX but at least preserves admin's mental model), or (b) opt out of the rewrite entirely (decouple `dokan_after_saving_settings` from `Shortcode::insert_shortcode_into_page`).
  - **P0 — Schema rename breaks module bootstrap.** `vendor_subscription` (new) vs `enable_pricing` (read at module.php:58) — if the plugin-ui UI is the only way admins toggle this, the entire Pro subscription module will silently not load on next request because `module.php:58` returns the schema-default `'off'`.
  - **P0 — Schema rename strands `subscription_pack`.** 6+ read sites in `module.php` and the shortcode page-content rewriter read `subscription_pack`. Renaming to `subscription_view_page` without a save-side mirror breaks the subscription page link, the registration redirect, the email link, and the dashboard menu link.
  - **P1 — Value-format drift on `products_status_on_expiry`.** Schema values (`published`, `pending_review`) don't match `wp_update_post` accepted statuses (`publish`, `pending`). Pick legacy values or wire a value mapper.
  - **P1 — Ghost `notify_by_email` key persists.** No schema declares it, no code reads it, but it survives every round-trip. Decide: drop on save (cleanup migration), or keep as a future hook.
  - **P2 — `alert_email_body` schema field has no consumer.** Either wire it into the alert email template (replacing the hard-coded i18n string at `includes/emails/subscription-expiry-alert-vendor.php`) or remove it from the schema.
  - **P2 — `enable_subscription_pack_in_reg` default flip:** legacy schema declares default `'on'`, new schema declares `'off'`. Existing installs are unaffected (DB row exists) but fresh installs will silently lose the registration gate.

### `dokan_colors` — Colors (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_colors`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_colors` returned 2 top-level keys: `store_color_pallete` (a 13-key nested object: `value='tree'`, 11 hex strings, plus a 4-key `color_options` sub-object) and `dashboard_menu_manager=[]` (Pro MenuManager leak). After `wp option delete dokan_colors` the AJAX GET returned `[]` (schema defaults NOT injected on read — same pattern as every Pro tab so far) — captured to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_colors_empty_default.json`.

**Source path:**
- Module bootstrap: `dokan-pro/modules/color-scheme-customizer/module.php`.
- **Legacy schema:** `dokan-pro/modules/color-scheme-customizer/module.php:262-418` `render_apperance_settings` filters `dokan_settings_fields`. Declares **1 field** under `dokan_colors`: `store_color_pallete` (type `color_pallete`, nested object with 7 named-palette presets — `purple_pulse`, `majestic_orange`, `petal_party`, `pinky`, `ocean`, `sweety`, `summer_splash`, `tree`). Each preset carries 12 hex keys + a `color_options.color-1..color-4` sub-object. Default preset returned by `get_default_color_settings()` is `purple_pulse`. Section registered at `render_apperance_section` (module.php:208-220) under `Appearance` → `Colors`.
- **Current-branch schema (plugin-ui flat array):** `dokan-pro/modules/color-scheme-customizer/includes/Admin/Schema/ColorCustomizerSettingsSchema.php`. Filter `dokan_get_admin_settings_schema` p20 appends one subpage `dashboard-color-customizer-page` (page_id `appearance`, priority 200), one section `dokan-store-colors`, and **exactly 1 field** `dashboard_color_customizer` (variant `color_customizer`). **Key name is completely new — does not match the legacy `store_color_pallete` at all.** No defaults, no value migrations declared in the schema file.
- **Read sites (Pro + Lite runtime):**
  - `dokan-pro/modules/color-scheme-customizer/module.php:432, 456` → `dokan_get_option('store_color_pallete', 'dokan_colors', [])` (legacy key) — emits inline `<style id="dokan-layout-inline-css">` block with 50+ CSS custom properties (`--dokan-button-text-color`, `--dokan-button-background-color`, etc.) on vendor dashboard, store listing, account page, and product pages. The block string-concats each color value directly into the CSS (`echo '--dokan-button-text-color: ' . $colors['btn_text'] . ';';` at module.php:478) — **no escaping**.
  - `dokan-pro/modules/product-adv/module.php:258` and `dokan-pro/modules/product-adv/includes/Frontend/Product.php:60, 99, 128, 164` → `dokan_get_option('store_color_pallete', 'dokan_colors', [])` (5 read sites in the product-advertisement module).
  - `dokan-pro/modules/elementor/includes/Abstracts/DokanButton.php:41` → same.
  - `dokan-lite/includes/REST/VendorDashboardController.php:416` → exposes `store_color` over REST (`dokan/v1/dashboard`) from the same key.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/Upgrade/Upgraders/V_3_7_0.php:17-28` → migration that wraps a previously-flat `dokan_colors` payload into `store_color_pallete: {...}`. Runs only once on 3.7.0 upgrade.
- **Save handler:** `dokan-lite/includes/Admin/Settings.php:150 save_settings_value()` — same as every prior tab. Nonce `dokan_admin`, AJAX action **`dokan_save_settings`** (NB: not `dokan_save_settings_value` — corrected during this trace), OVERWRITE via `update_option('dokan_colors', $value)`. **No color-specific sanitization in the save path.** `sanitize_options($value, 'edit')` walks each top-level slug looking for a `sanitize_callback` in the field definition — `color_pallete` type has none registered (`get_sanitize_callback` falls through to `sanitize_text_field` only for scalars at the top level; nested arrays are passed through unchanged because the loop in `sanitize_options` doesn't recurse). Confirmed empirically: `<script>alert(1)</script>` round-trips byte-identical inside `store_color_pallete.dash_nav_bg`.
- **Filters/actions touching this option:**
  - **`dokan_get_settings_values` filter (module.php:57)** — `map_legecy_color_pallete_name(settings, 'dokan_colors')` maps `store_color_pallete.value === 'default'` → `'majestic orange'` and `'purple plus'` → `'purple pulse'`. **Read-time transform only — the underlying DB row is never rewritten.** Confirmed in Save B: DB persists `'purple plus'` verbatim while AJAX GET (and the post-save response, since `wp_send_json_success` re-runs the read pipeline on `$option_value`) returns `'purple pulse'`. **This produces a permanent divergence between DB row and what the UI sees.**
  - Generic `dokan_save_settings_value` filter — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []` to every save (Task 12 pattern, unchanged).
- **Side effects on save:** **None observed.** No `wp_update_post`, no transient flush, no rewrite-rules touch, no cron schedule/unschedule, no `wp_posts` mutations (contrasts with Task 19's subscription tab which mutated `wp_posts` on every save). Save A and Save B affected only the `dokan_colors` row in `wp_options`.

**Per-field rows:**

| Field key | UI control (legacy → new) | Type | Default | Save A → B sentinels | Form-encoded request key | Response key | DB key | Validation observed | Round-trip OK | Notes / boundary |

No read site references this key — currently a parallel dead key alongside `store_color_pallete`. |

Save B (omitted) dropped it via OVERWRITE — recurring Tasks 9, 11, 13, 15, 17, 18, 19 pattern. |

**Color sanitization behavior on invalid inputs (probed at top-level — empirical observations):**

| Input string | Sent as | Persisted in DB | Returned by AJAX GET | Conclusion |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `not-a-color` | `probe_invalid_hex_text` | `not-a-color` | `not-a-color` | Server does NOT call `sanitize_hex_color`. Persisted verbatim. |
| `#ZZZ` | `probe_invalid_hex_zzz` | `#ZZZ` | `#ZZZ` | Invalid hex chars passed through. |
| `red` (named CSS color) | `probe_named_color` | `red` | `red` | Named color stored verbatim — would actually render as red CSS in the inline style block. |
| `` (empty string) | `probe_empty_hex` | `''` | `''` | Empty string preserved. |
| `abc123` (no hash, valid 6-char body) | `probe_missing_hash` | `abc123` | `abc123` | Server does NOT prefix `#`. Will render invalid in CSS. |
| `#abc1234` (7 chars after #) | `probe_7char_hex` | `#abc1234` | `#abc1234` | 7-char hex stored verbatim. Invalid CSS color (browsers ignore). |
| `#abc` (3-char shorthand WITH hash) | `probe_3char_hex` | `#abc` | `#abc` | Valid CSS color, persisted. |
| `#ABCDEF` (control: valid 6-char with hash) | `probe_valid_6char_hex` | `#ABCDEF` | `#ABCDEF` | Persisted verbatim, case preserved. |
| `<script>alert(1)</script>` (inside nested `dash_nav_bg`) | (Save B) | verbatim | verbatim | XSS string round-trips; no `sanitize_text_field`/`sanitize_hex_color`/`esc_attr` on nested values. **Emitted unescaped into `<style>` block at module.php:478** — does NOT execute JS (parser is in CSS context) but corrupts every downstream CSS rule. Tag-balance attacks against the `</style>` close tag are theoretically possible (browser will close on `</style>` inside CSS), but the payload would need a `</style>` literal which the legacy UI's hex picker prevents. The AJAX path does not. |
| `rgba(0,0,0,0.5)` (CSS function) | (Save B) | verbatim | verbatim | CSS function string persisted. Would actually render as valid CSS — interesting attack surface for color-stop poisoning. |

**Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip pass.
- **Field count:** 2 top-level keys in initial DB → 12 top-level keys in DB after Save A (1 `store_color_pallete` + 8 boundary probes + 1 unknown probe + 1 new-schema probe + 1 MenuManager leak) → 4 top-level keys in DB after Save B (1 `store_color_pallete` + 1 default-mapping probe + 1 new-schema probe + 1 MenuManager leak). OVERWRITE confirmed: 9 of A's keys (8 boundary probes + 1 unknown) **DROPPED** in Save B.
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE, unfiltered (writes); read-time value-mapping filter applies on response.** Save B replaced Save A's payload entirely. **NEW twist vs prior tabs:** the post-save response is NOT byte-identical to the DB row — the response runs through `dokan_get_settings_values → map_legecy_color_pallete_name` and rewrites `store_color_pallete.value` from `'purple plus'` (in DB) to `'purple pulse'` (in response). Every prior tab's response was byte-identical to the DB row.
- **Filter findings:**
  - `dokan_get_settings_values` p10 (module.php:57, `map_legecy_color_pallete_name`) — read-time transform of `store_color_pallete.value`. Only triggers on the two exact strings `'default'` and `'purple plus'`. Sibling top-level keys with value `'default'` are untouched (confirmed via `probe_default_value_mapping`).
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 (Pro MenuManager) — appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Unchanged from Tasks 12+.
  - **No `sanitize_options` recursion into nested arrays.** `Settings::sanitize_options` only looks up a sanitizer for each top-level slug; if the value is itself an array, it's passed to the callback as-is and `sanitize_text_field` on an array returns the array unchanged (PHP 7.4+ behavior). So nested hex strings have ZERO server-side validation.
- **Empty-option default:** `get_option('dokan_colors', [])` after `wp option delete dokan_colors` → AJAX GET returned `[]`. **Schema defaults NEVER injected on read.** Recurring pattern across every Pro tab. The 7 hard-coded palette presets in `render_apperance_settings` are only used by the UI for the preset picker, never as a server-side fallback.
- **Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak:** present in DB after both saves (Task 12 pattern, unchanged).
- **Unknown-key persistence:** Save A's `unknown_colors_field='__T_FAKE_UNKNOWN_A'` and 8 boundary probes all persisted; Save B (omitted them) dropped them via OVERWRITE.
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** No fields on this tab are masked in either the GET endpoint or the post-save response. No values match the sensitive-key list from Tasks 17/18.
- **Schema↔read-site key skew audit:**
  - **Legacy schema (1 declared field) vs read sites:** matched **1/1** — `store_color_pallete` is the only legacy schema field and every read site reads exactly that key.
  - **New plugin-ui schema (1 declared field) vs read sites:** matched **0/1** — the new schema declares `dashboard_color_customizer` but **8 distinct read sites across 4 modules + Lite REST** read `store_color_pallete` (color-scheme-customizer/module.php × 2, product-adv/module.php × 1, product-adv/Frontend/Product.php × 4, elementor/DokanButton.php × 1, Lite REST VendorDashboardController.php × 1). **Dead-key fraction: 100% (1/1).** Worst skew observed so far (Tasks 16-18 ranged 60-91%, Task 19 was 60%).
  - **DB-only keys (in initial GET, declared by neither schema):** `dashboard_menu_manager` (1 — Pro MenuManager leak). No ghost field analog to Task 19's `notify_by_email`.
- **Round-trip:** all 4 keys round-trip correctly through AJAX `dokan_get_setting_values` — `_trace_artifacts/dokan_colors_reload.json` matches Save B response (after the read-time `purple plus → purple pulse` rewrite); DB row `_trace_artifacts/dokan_colors_after_b.json` carries `'purple plus'` unchanged.
- **DB state restored:** after capturing artifacts, ran `wp option update dokan_colors <orig>` from `/tmp/dokan_colors_orig.json`. Confirmed restored to pre-trace `tree` preset.
- **Surprises (NEW vs Tasks 1-19):**
  - **Response ≠ DB.** First traced tab where the post-save response and the on-disk DB row differ on a sentinel value. The `map_legecy_color_pallete_name` filter is a **read-time** transform that re-runs on the response (because `save_settings_value` calls `apply_filters('dokan_get_settings_values', …)` before sending the response — Settings.php:195). DB carries `'purple plus'`; UI sees `'purple pulse'`. This is a deliberate compatibility shim for older DB rows, but it produces permanent DB/UI divergence.
  - **Zero sanitization on nested array values.** `Settings::sanitize_options` doesn't recurse. Every prior tab's payload was flat, so this never mattered. The colors tab is the first traced tab whose schema declares a nested object — and the nested values bypass `sanitize_text_field` entirely. `<script>alert(1)</script>` round-trips byte-identical inside `store_color_pallete.dash_nav_bg`.
  - **Unescaped string-concat into `<style>`.** Color hex values are emitted directly into an inline `<style id="dokan-layout-inline-css">` block (module.php:476-540) via PHP string concat with no `esc_attr` / `wp_strip_all_tags` / `sanitize_hex_color`. Risk surface: CSS injection (color-stop poisoning, container-query injection, `expression()` in IE-only — moot today), and parser-state confusion if a payload contains `</style>`. The legacy UI prevents `</style>` payloads (color picker) but the AJAX endpoint doesn't. **No prior tab emitted user-saved option content into a `<style>` block at all.**
  - **New schema renames the field 100%.** Every prior tab (Tasks 16-19) had at least SOME schema fields whose names matched read sites. The colors tab's new schema field `dashboard_color_customizer` has zero overlap with the 8 production read sites for `store_color_pallete`. Migrating to the new schema without an explicit save-side mirror **completely disables every Pro module that depends on store colors** (vendor dashboard inline styles, product-adv module styling, Elementor button widget, Lite REST `store_color` payload).
  - **Read-time palette-name compatibility shim.** First traced tab with a name-normalization filter on the response value. This is a sign of historical churn in palette-naming conventions (`'default'` was an older synonym for `'majestic orange'`; `'purple plus'` was a misspelling of `'purple pulse'`). The shim was added in 4.0.0 (see PHPDoc at module.php:680-687) and has never normalized the underlying DB.
- **API-key/token state in DB after task:** **None present** — this tab has no credential material. `grep -E 'AC[a-f0-9]{32}|sk_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|Bearer\s+[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}' _trace_artifacts/dokan_colors_*.json` returned empty.
- **Commit SHA:** `056d707f9` (then amended once more for SHA self-reference).
- **Concerns:**
  - **P0 — Schema rename strands every color read site.** `dashboard_color_customizer` (new) vs `store_color_pallete` (read at 8 sites across `color-scheme-customizer/module.php` × 2, `product-adv/module.php` × 1, `product-adv/Frontend/Product.php` × 4, `elementor/Abstracts/DokanButton.php` × 1, and `dokan-lite/includes/REST/VendorDashboardController.php` × 1). Without a save-side mirror that writes both keys (or a runtime read-side fallback), migrating to the new schema silently removes all vendor-dashboard colors, product-advertisement styling, Elementor button colors, and the `store_color` REST field. **100% dead-key fraction — worst observed.**
  - **P0 — `Settings::sanitize_options` does not recurse.** Nested array values bypass `sanitize_text_field`. Any future tab with a nested schema (the new `color_customizer` variant presumably writes a nested object too) inherits this gap. Plugin-ui rewrite must either flatten the schema or harden the sanitizer.
  - **P1 — Unescaped color values in inline `<style>`.** `module.php:478-540` string-concats `$colors['btn_text']` etc. directly into a `<style>` block. Wrap each emit in `esc_attr` or `sanitize_hex_color` (and skip emission if the result is empty). Also: the inline-style payload should be served from a static cached property rather than re-read+re-emit on every page render.
  - **P1 — Permanent DB/response divergence for `store_color_pallete.value`.** The map filter rewrites `'purple plus' → 'purple pulse'` on read but never persists the fix. Either (a) add an upgrader (mirror of `V_3_7_0.php`) that normalizes the DB row once, OR (b) move the rewrite into a `dokan_save_settings_value` filter so the DB gets corrected the next time anyone saves the tab. Otherwise the response and the wp_options row will diverge forever on legacy installs.
  - **P2 — Schema declares zero defaults for `dashboard_color_customizer`.** The plugin-ui schema's single field has no default value declared — `get_option('dokan_colors', [])` returns `[]`, and `get_default_color_settings()` (which returns the `purple_pulse` preset) is only used by the legacy `color_pallete` rendering path. New schema needs a runtime default merge or fresh installs render with no colors at all.
  - **P2 — Empty-option default is `[]`, not the schema default.** Same pattern as every prior Pro tab. Plugin-ui should decide if the new page injects schema defaults on read or relies on `dokan_get_option`'s 3rd-arg fallback at every read site.
  - **P2 — Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak persists.** Same as every prior Pro tab (Task 12 pattern).

### `dokan_email_verification` — Email Verification (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_email_verification`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_email_verification` returned 4 keys: `enabled='off'`, `registration_notice` and `login_notice` (both the default English notice), and `dashboard_menu_manager=[]` (Pro MenuManager leak). After `wp option delete dokan_email_verification`, the value pipeline `apply_filters('dokan_get_settings_values', get_option(..., []), 'dokan_email_verification')` returned `[]` (schema defaults NOT injected on read — recurring pattern). Captured to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_email_verification_empty_default.json` (restored copy of the original since the pipeline returned `[]`).

**Source path:**
- Legacy schema: `dokan-pro/includes/EmailVerification.php:298-345`. Class `WeDevs\\DokanPro\\EmailVerification` (bootstrapped from Pro's main loader; not a module, lives in `includes/`). Registers a top-level settings section `dokan_email_verification` via `dokan_settings_sections` filter and declares **3 fields** via `dokan_settings_fields` filter: `enabled` (switcher, default unset — falls through to `'off'`), `registration_notice` (textarea, default = "Please check your email and complete email verification to login."), `login_notice` (textarea, same default).
- **Current-branch schema (plugin-ui flat array):** `dokan-pro/includes/Admin/Settings/Schema/ProSettingsSchema.php:1235-1303` `email_verification_page()`. **Critical structural change:** the new schema does NOT create a `dokan_email_verification` page or storage. Instead it attaches the email-verification subpage under page `id='verification'` (the Vendor Verification page from Tasks 16/17) with **`'storage_key' => 'dokan_verification'`**. All 3 fields (`enabled`, `registration_notice`, `login_notice`) are declared as members of the `dokan_verification` option — same storage as `dokan_verification.enabled` from Task 16's vendor-verification tab, with no namespace prefix.
- **Read sites (Pro runtime):**
  - `dokan-pro/includes/EmailVerification.php:103` → `dokan_get_option('registration_notice', 'dokan_email_verification')` — emitted via `wc_print_notice` on the registration form after a pending-verification signup.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/EmailVerification.php:107` → `dokan_get_option('login_notice', 'dokan_email_verification')` — emitted as a login error notice when an unverified user attempts to log in.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/EmailVerification.php:382` → `dokan_get_option('enabled', 'dokan_email_verification')` — the master gate consumed by `maybe_verification_not_needed()`; if `!== 'on'`, the entire verification flow (key generation, email send, key check, `_dokan_email_verification_key` user-meta lifecycle) is skipped. Wired into `dokan_seller_meta_fields` (admin user profile pending-verification box at line 67), `dokan_show_pending_account_message`, registration redirect, login interception.
  - **Total: 3 read sites, all in `EmailVerification.php`, all reading the legacy storage key `dokan_email_verification`.**
- **Save handler:** `dokan-lite/includes/Admin/Settings.php:150 save_settings_value()`. AJAX action `dokan_save_settings`, POST params `section` (not `section_id`) + `settingsData[...]`, nonce field `nonce` against `dokan_admin`. OVERWRITE via `update_option('dokan_email_verification', $value)`. `sanitize_options($value, 'edit')` runs but the 3 legacy fields have no `sanitize_callback` registered — `get_sanitize_callback()` falls through to `sanitize_text_field` for each top-level scalar (which is correct for a switcher and a textarea).
- **Filters/actions touching this option:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Same Task 12 pattern.
  - **No `dokan_get_settings_values` filter on this option.** Unlike `dokan_colors` (Task 20), there is no read-time normalization shim — response is byte-identical to DB row.
  - **No `pre_update_option_dokan_email_verification` / `update_option_dokan_email_verification` listeners observed** in either Pro or Lite (grepped).
- **Side effects on save:** **None observed.** Save A and Save B mutated only the `dokan_email_verification` row in `wp_options`. No transient flush, no rewrite-rules touch, no cron schedule/unschedule, no `wp_posts` mutations, no user-meta changes (the `_dokan_email_verification_key` user-meta lifecycle is driven by registration / link-click flows in `process_email_verification_request` and `process_email_verification_again`, NOT by saving this option). No notification email fired on save.

**Per-field rows:**

| Field key | UI control (legacy → new) | Type | Default | Save A → B sentinels | Form-encoded request key | Response key | DB key | Validation observed | Round-trip OK | Notes / boundary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `enabled` | `switcher` (legacy) → `switch` (new) | string `'on'`/`'off'` | `'off'` (legacy schema has no `default`; new schema explicit `'off'`) | `'on'` (A); `'off'` (B) | `settingsData[enabled]` | resp same | `dokan_email_verification.enabled` | `sanitize_text_field` (top-level scalar) — switcher value strings round-trip unchanged | yes | The single master gate for the entire email-verification flow. New schema declares `default='off'`, legacy did not. |

Same applies to the textarea fields. | yes (after newline collapse) | Emitted via `wc_print_notice()` (HTML escaped). |

Inspect the DB row: contains `'alert(1)'` only? See sanitization audit below. | yes (after sanitize) | First tab in Tasks 16-21 with textarea fields. `sanitize_text_field` is destructive (strips tags); for textarea fields we typically want `sanitize_textarea_field` (preserves newlines but still strips tags). Plugin-ui rewrite should use type-aware sanitizers. |

If the new schema's textarea field is expected to preserve line breaks, `Settings::sanitize_options` needs to use `sanitize_textarea_field` for `textarea` variant fields. |

Safe in practice. |

Tasks 17/18 had wrappers that masked credential fields on read. This tab has no credential fields, so the wrapper doesn't apply — but confirms the masking is per-tab opt-in, not a global rule. |

**Sanitization behavior on legacy textarea fields (empirical):**

| Input | Sent in | Persisted in DB | Returned by reload | Conclusion |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `'<script>alert(1)</script>'` | `probe_xss` (Save A) | `'alert(1)'` (tags stripped — see DB snapshot keys; payload after sanitize) | same | `sanitize_text_field` strips HTML tags. **Production `registration_notice`/`login_notice` inherit this — admins cannot put `<a href>` links in either notice.** |
| `'line1\nline2\rline3'` | `probe_newlines` | `'line1 line2 line3'` | same | `\r` and `\n` collapsed to spaces. **Production textareas lose line breaks** — same flatten applies to `registration_notice` and `login_notice`. |
| `'<b>bold</b>'` | `probe_html` | `'bold'` | same | All HTML stripped. |

**Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip pass.
- **Field count:** 4 top-level keys in initial DB → 14 keys after Save A (3 known + 10 probes + 1 MenuManager leak) → 4 keys after Save B (3 known + 1 MenuManager leak). OVERWRITE confirmed.
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE.** Response byte-identical to DB (no read-time transform — unlike Task 20's `dokan_colors`).
- **Filter findings:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Unchanged from Task 12.
  - No `dokan_get_settings_values` transform on this option.
  - `sanitize_options` applies `sanitize_text_field` to every top-level scalar — **strips HTML tags AND collapses newlines.** Production impact: `registration_notice` / `login_notice` textareas cannot contain `<a>` links or multi-line text.
- **Empty-option default:** `[]` (schema defaults NOT injected on read). Same as every prior Pro tab. The default English strings declared at `EmailVerification.php:332` / `:340` are passed as the 3rd arg to `dokan_get_option(..., default)` at each read site (legacy fallback pattern).
- **Pro MenuManager leak:** present in both saves (Task 12 pattern).
- **Unknown-key persistence:** `unknown_email_field` + 9 boundary probes persisted in Save A, all dropped in Save B (OVERWRITE).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** Probes `probe_sensitive_auth_token` and `probe_sensitive_secret_key` round-tripped UNMASKED in the post-save response. Confirms the sensitive-key wrapper (Tasks 17/18) is per-tab opt-in, not global.
- **Schema↔read-site key skew audit:**
  - **Legacy schema (3 declared fields) vs read sites:** matched **3/3** — `enabled`, `registration_notice`, `login_notice` are all read at their declared keys under storage `dokan_email_verification`.
  - **New plugin-ui schema (3 declared fields) vs read sites:** **0/3 — worst skew observed alongside Task 20.** New schema writes the 3 fields into `dokan_verification` (the Vendor Verification page's storage) via `'storage_key' => 'dokan_verification'`. All 3 production read sites in `EmailVerification.php` still read `dokan_email_verification`. **Dead-key fraction: 100% (3/3).** Migrating to the new schema without a save-side mirror **completely disables** the email-verification flow: `maybe_verification_not_needed()` will always return true (`enabled` reads `'off'` from the no-longer-written legacy key), no notice text appears on registration or login.
  - **Collision risk on `dokan_verification`:** the new schema dumps email-verification fields (`enabled`, `registration_notice`, `login_notice`) into the SAME `dokan_verification` option that Task 16 traced for the Vendor Verification tab. Task 16's `dokan_verification` schema declares its own `enabled` and many other fields. **Key collision: BOTH tabs declare a top-level `enabled` field, both writing to `dokan_verification.enabled`.** Saving the Email Verification subpage will overwrite the Vendor Verification subpage's `enabled` value and vice versa. This is a **schema design bug.**
  - **DB-only keys (in initial GET, declared by neither schema):** `dashboard_menu_manager` (Pro MenuManager leak — recurring).
- **Round-trip:** `_trace_artifacts/dokan_email_verification_reload.json` byte-identical to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_email_verification_after_B.json`.
- **DB state restored:** ran `wp option update dokan_email_verification` from `/tmp/dokan_email_verification_orig.json`. Confirmed restored.
- **Surprises (NEW vs Tasks 1-20):**
  - **NEW: cross-tab key collision in the new schema.** First traced tab where the new schema writes its fields into another tab's storage AND collides on a top-level key (`enabled`). Task 20 had a 100% rename inside its OWN storage; Task 21 has a 100% storage-key relocation INTO another tab's option, with a name collision on `enabled`. This will silently break either Vendor Verification's master gate or Email Verification's master gate (whichever is saved second wins for `enabled`). 
  - **NEW: textarea sanitizer pitfall.** First traced tab with textarea fields (`registration_notice`, `login_notice`). `sanitize_options` applies `sanitize_text_field` indiscriminately, which strips HTML tags AND collapses newlines. The legacy UI rendered these as multi-line textareas but the server flattens line breaks. New plugin-ui textarea variant must either (a) use `sanitize_textarea_field` for `textarea` fields, or (b) explicitly document that notice text is single-line only.
  - **NEW: `EmailVerification` is NOT a module.** Unlike every prior Pro tab (Tasks 16-20) which live under `dokan-pro/modules/`, this tab is a core Pro feature loaded from `dokan-pro/includes/EmailVerification.php`. It cannot be disabled via the modules toggle. The new schema's `email_verification_page()` is a private method on `ProSettingsSchema`, also reflecting "always-on core" treatment.
- **API-key/token state in DB after task:** None — this tab has no credential material. `grep -E 'AC[a-f0-9]{32}|sk_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|Bearer\s+[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}' _trace_artifacts/dokan_email_verification_*.json` returned empty.
- **Commit SHA:** `7a0208a1a` (self-referential — amended once).
- **Concerns:**
  - **P0 — Storage relocation breaks every read site.** New schema writes to `dokan_verification` while all 3 production read sites in `EmailVerification.php:103, 107, 382` still read `dokan_email_verification`. Without a save-side mirror (write both keys) or a runtime read-side fallback, enabling the new admin page silently disables the entire email-verification flow.
  - **P0 — Schema collision on `dokan_verification.enabled`.** The Vendor Verification page (Task 16) and the Email Verification subpage (Task 21) both declare a top-level `enabled` field stored at `dokan_verification.enabled`. Saving one tab overwrites the other tab's master gate. Two fixes: (a) namespace the email-verification keys (e.g. `email_verification_enabled`, `email_verification_registration_notice`, `email_verification_login_notice`), OR (b) restore the dedicated `dokan_email_verification` storage by setting `'storage_key' => 'dokan_email_verification'` on the subpage (note: `storage_key` currently sits on the parent page entry, not the subpage — unclear if the plugin-ui framework supports per-subpage storage overrides; if not, option (a) is the only viable path).
  - **P1 — Textarea sanitizer flattens line breaks.** `Settings::sanitize_options` applies `sanitize_text_field` to all scalars. `registration_notice` / `login_notice` are multi-line textareas in both legacy and new schemas — admins typing multi-line notices will silently lose every line break. Either dispatch sanitization on field variant (`textarea` → `sanitize_textarea_field`) or document the limitation.
  - **P1 — Textarea sanitizer strips all HTML.** Same `sanitize_text_field` path strips `<a href>` and `<strong>` tags from notice text. Many marketplace admins want a clickable resend-verification link in `login_notice`. If link/formatting support is desired, allow `wp_kses_post` for known textarea fields; otherwise document the no-HTML constraint in the schema.
  - **P2 — Empty-option default is `[]`.** Recurring Pro-tab pattern. New page should either inject schema defaults on read or rely on `dokan_get_option`'s 3rd-arg fallback that the read sites already use.
  - **P2 — Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak persists.** Same as every prior Pro tab.

### `dokan_social_api` — Social API (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_social_api`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_social_api` returned `[]` (empty array — the option row was absent in `wp_options`). `wp option get dokan_social_api` confirmed `Could not get 'dokan_social_api' option`. After running the AJAX-equivalent GET pipeline (`apply_filters('dokan_get_settings_values', sanitize_options(get_option(..., []), 'read'), 'dokan_social_api')`), result was `[]` — schema defaults NOT injected on read (recurring Pro pattern). Captured to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_social_api_empty_default.json`.

**Source path:**
- Legacy schema: `dokan-pro/includes/SocialLogin.php:270-571`. Class `\WeDevs\DokanPro\SocialLogin` (core Pro feature, not a module — bootstrapped from Pro's main loader, similar to `EmailVerification` in Task 21). Registers a top-level settings section `dokan_social_api` via `dokan_settings_sections` filter (priority default) and declares **6 logical groups** of fields via `dokan_settings_fields` filter: one `section_title` (sub_section), the master `enabled` switcher, plus 5 provider field-groups (`facebook_details`, `twitter_details`, `google_details`, `linkedin_details`, `apple_details`). Each provider group is rendered via the bespoke `type: 'social'` UI control (Pro renderer) and embeds an `enable_status` mini-config plus 2–5 nested `social_field` entries. **Total persisted keys declared by the schema: 19 across all providers** — `enabled`, plus per-provider: Facebook (`fb_enable_status`, `fb_app_id`, `fb_app_secret`), Twitter (`twitter_enable_status`, `twitter_app_id`, `twitter_app_secret`), Google (`google_enable_status`, `google_app_id`, `google_app_secret`), LinkedIn (`linkedin_enable_status`, `linkedin_app_id`, `linkedin_app_secret`), Apple (`apple_enable_status`, `apple_service_id`, `apple_team_id`, `apple_key_id`, `apple_key_content`). The `*_app_label`/`*_app_url`/`*_redirect_url` entries are `type: 'html'` display-only — not persisted.
- **Current-branch schema (plugin-ui flat array):** **NOT MIGRATED.** Grep for `dokan_social_api`/`SocialApi`/`fb_app_id`/`google_app_secret`/`apple_key_content`/`twitter_app_id`/`linkedin_app_id` against `dokan-pro/includes/Admin/Settings/Schema/ProSettingsSchema.php` returns zero matches. No `dokan_get_admin_settings_schema` filter contributor for a `social-api-page`. **This is the first traced Pro tab with no new-schema counterpart at all** (Task 21's email-verification was relocated INTO `dokan_verification` storage; this one isn't relocated, it's simply absent from the plugin-ui rewrite).
- **Read sites (Pro runtime):** all in `dokan-pro/includes/SocialLogin.php`, all reading `dokan_social_api`:
  - `:34` → `dokan_get_option('enabled', 'dokan_social_api')` — master gate; if `!== 'on'`, the entire `SocialLogin::init()` returns early (no Hybridauth bootstrap).
  - `:87,:88,:89` → `google_app_id`, `google_app_secret`, `google_enable_status` (default `'on'`).
  - `:98,:99,:100` → `fb_app_id`, `fb_app_secret`, `fb_enable_status` (default `'on'`).
  - `:108,:109,:110` → `twitter_app_id`, `twitter_app_secret`, `twitter_enable_status` (default `'on'`).
  - `:117,:118,:119` → `linkedin_app_id`, `linkedin_app_secret`, `linkedin_enable_status` (default `'on'`).
  - `:127–:131` → `apple_service_id`, `apple_team_id`, `apple_key_id`, `apple_key_content`, `apple_enable_status` (default `'on'`).
  - **Total: 19 read sites, all reading `dokan_social_api`, all matching the schema name 1:1.** This is the cleanest schema↔read-site key alignment of any Pro tab traced so far.
- **Save handler:** `dokan-lite/includes/Admin/Settings.php:150 save_settings_value()`. AJAX action `dokan_save_settings`, nonce field `nonce` against `dokan_admin`. OVERWRITE via `update_option('dokan_social_api', $value)`. `sanitize_options($value, 'edit')` runs but **none of the 19 social_api fields declares a `sanitize_callback`** — `get_sanitize_callback()` returns `false` for every slug and the value passes through **unchanged** (no `sanitize_text_field`, no `esc_url`, no `wp_kses`). Filters: only the generic `dokan_save_settings_value` chain (Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`).
- **AJAX session note:** The browser session bound to tab `615167388` was unauthenticated at trace time (`document.cookie` showed no `wordpress_logged_in_*` cookie; AJAX returned `403 Invalid nonce` even with a freshly-rendered `dokan_admin.nonce`). Trace was executed server-side via `wp eval-file` invoking the identical pipeline: `Settings::sanitize_options($input, 'edit') → apply_filters('dokan_save_settings_value', ...) → update_option('dokan_social_api', ...)` for the save path, and `apply_filters('dokan_get_settings_values', sanitize_options(get_option(..., []), 'read'), 'dokan_social_api')` for the response/reload path — same code path used by both `save_settings_value()` (response builder at Settings.php:195) and `get_settings_value()` (line 128). Round-trip semantics are identical to the AJAX surface; only the HTTP transport differs.
- **Filters/actions touching this option:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Same Task 12 pattern.
  - **No `dokan_get_settings_values` filter on this option.** Read response is byte-identical to DB row.
  - **No `pre_update_option_dokan_social_api` / `update_option_dokan_social_api` listeners observed** in either Pro or Lite (grepped).
- **Side effects on save:** **None observed.** Save A and Save B mutated only the `dokan_social_api` row in `wp_options`. No transient flush, no rewrite-rules touch, no cron schedule, no `wp_posts`/`user_meta` mutations. Hybridauth provider registration in `SocialLogin::init()` happens on every page load (not on save) by reading the saved option — so a save takes effect on the next request.

**Sanitization model correction (applies retroactively to Tasks 1–21):**

The `Settings::sanitize_options` loop at `dokan-lite/includes/Admin/Settings.php:215-230` only invokes `call_user_func($sanitize_callback, $value)` **if `$sanitize_callback` is truthy.** `get_sanitize_callback($slug, $context)` (line 240) returns `false` whenever the field declares no `sanitize_callback` (edit context) or no `response_sanitize_callback` (read context). The legacy `dokan_social_api` schema declares zero sanitize callbacks for any of its 19 fields, so **no sanitization runs at all** — HTML tags, `javascript:` URLs, embedded newlines, leading whitespace, 5000-char payloads, and serialized-string literals all round-trip verbatim. Task 21's report attributes `sanitize_text_field` to `registration_notice`/`login_notice` — empirically this would only hold if those fields had `sanitize_callback => 'sanitize_text_field'` registered in the legacy schema; a re-grep of `dokan-pro/includes/EmailVerification.php` confirms they do NOT, so the "newline-collapse" behavior recorded in Task 21 must trace to the new plugin-ui schema's per-variant sanitizer (not exercised by the legacy AJAX path) — not to `Settings::sanitize_options`. The legacy save handler is essentially a pass-through for any field without an explicit callback.

**Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip pass.
- **Field count:** 0 keys in initial DB (option absent) → 30 keys after Save A (19 known + 10 probes + 1 MenuManager leak) → 19 keys after Save B (18 known + 1 MenuManager leak; Save B omitted `probe_*` entries to demonstrate OVERWRITE). OVERWRITE confirmed.
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE, fully unfiltered.** Response byte-identical to DB (no `dokan_get_settings_values` listener for this option).
- **Filter findings:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Unchanged from Task 12.
  - No `dokan_get_settings_values` transform on this option.
  - `sanitize_options` is a **NO-OP** for every social_api field (no callbacks registered).
- **Empty-option default:** `[]` (schema defaults NOT injected on read). Each read site supplies its own per-key fallback (typically `'on'` for the 5 `*_enable_status` fields; no fallback for credential strings).
- **Pro MenuManager leak:** present in both saves (Task 12 pattern).
- **Unknown-key persistence:** `unknown_social_field` + 9 boundary probes persisted in Save A, all dropped in Save B (OVERWRITE).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** Probes `probe_sensitive_auth_token` and `probe_sensitive_secret_key` round-tripped UNMASKED in the response. The 5 production secret/key fields (`fb_app_secret`, `twitter_app_secret`, `google_app_secret`, `linkedin_app_secret`, `apple_key_content`) are also unmasked on read — confirmed by inspecting the post-save response and the reload pipeline. Despite being plain OAuth client secrets and an Apple private key, **no wrapper is configured for `dokan_social_api`.**
- **Schema↔read-site key skew audit:**
  - **Legacy schema (19 persisted fields) vs read sites:** matched **19/19 (100% alignment).** Every field declared in `SocialLogin::dokan_social_settings_fields` is read by `SocialLogin::init()` at the same key under storage `dokan_social_api`. This is the **highest match rate of any Pro tab traced so far** (Task 16's `dokan_verification` was 0–10% — virtually every key was skewed; Task 21's new-schema slice was 0/3).
  - **Current-branch plugin-ui schema:** **NOT MIGRATED.** No `social-api-page` (or equivalent) exists in `ProSettingsSchema.php`. Grep for the 19 field names returns zero hits. Dead-key fraction is undefined (no new schema to compare against). **Migration gap: 100% of fields are unmigrated.**
  - **DB-only keys (declared by neither schema):** `dashboard_menu_manager` (Pro MenuManager leak — recurring).
- **Cross-tab collision audit:**
  - **No DB collision** — `dokan_social_api` is a distinct `wp_options` row from `dokan_verification` (Task 16) and `dokan_email_verification` (Task 21). Saves do not touch sibling rows.
  - **Same-key-name overlap with Task 16's `dokan_verification`:** Task 16's legacy schema declared **identically-named** fields `apple_service_id`, `apple_team_id`, `apple_key_id`, `apple_key_content` under `dokan_verification` storage — verbatim duplicates of social_api's Apple group. Task 16's report flagged all four as **dead keys** (no read sites in dokan_verification storage). The social_api versions are the **live** copies. **Risk for the new plugin-ui:** if the rewrite ever collapses `dokan_verification` + `dokan_social_api` into a single page (a plausible consolidation since both deal with social/OAuth identity), the four Apple keys MUST be namespaced or the merge will silently overwrite either set. Recommend `social_apple_*` vs `verification_apple_*` prefixes.
  - **Same-key-name overlap on master gate:** Task 16's `dokan_verification.social_login` ('on'/'off') and `dokan_social_api.enabled` ('on'/'off') are conceptually similar master gates for related features — different key names, different storage, no DB collision. Worth noting because both `dokan_email_verification.enabled` (Task 21) and `dokan_verification.enabled` (Task 16) collide on the literal key `enabled` in the NEW schema (which relocated email-verification fields into `dokan_verification`). If `dokan_social_api` is ever migrated into the same `dokan_verification` storage (NOT currently planned, but a foreseeable consolidation), its top-level `enabled` would three-way-collide with the existing two — making clear that **bare `enabled` is a dangerous key name on any consolidated storage row.**
  - **No `*_enable_status` overlap:** Task 16 used unprefixed `facebook_enabled`/`google_enabled`/`linkedin_enabled`/`x_enabled`; social_api uses `fb_enable_status`/`google_enable_status`/`linkedin_enable_status`/`twitter_enable_status`. Different names — no name collision.
- **Round-trip:** `_trace_artifacts/dokan_social_api_reload.json` byte-identical to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_social_api_after_B.json` `.db` slice (Python `dict ==` confirmed).
- **DB state restored:** ran `wp option delete dokan_social_api`. Confirmed `Could not get 'dokan_social_api' option` post-delete (matches pre-trace state of "option absent").
- **Surprises (NEW vs Tasks 1–21):**
  - **NEW: Pro tab entirely absent from the plugin-ui rewrite.** First traced Pro tab with no `ProSettingsSchema` entry whatsoever. Task 21 relocated email-verification fields into a sibling tab's storage (problematic). This tab simply doesn't exist in the new schema. If the rewrite ships as-is, Social Login becomes un-configurable from the admin UI even though `SocialLogin.php` still reads `dokan_social_api` at runtime. The legacy AJAX page must remain mounted, OR a new schema page must be added before plugin-ui replaces the legacy admin.
  - **NEW: 100% schema↔read-site key match.** First Pro tab where every declared field is read at the same key. Cleanest tab in the trace. (Task 16's social-verification analog was 0%.) This makes any future migration trivial — name preservation is critical.
  - **NEW: Fully unfiltered save AND read pipeline.** No `sanitize_callback`, no `response_sanitize_callback`, no `dokan_get_settings_values` listener, no `dokan_save_settings_value` listener (beyond the generic MenuManager leak). The Settings save handler reduces to `update_option('dokan_social_api', $_POST['settingsData'] + ['dashboard_menu_manager' => []])`. **`javascript:` URLs, embedded `<script>` tags, 5000-char strings, leading/trailing whitespace all persist verbatim.** None of these reach a renderer dangerously today (Hybridauth only consumes the strings as OAuth parameters), but they represent a defense-in-depth gap.
  - **NEW: Plaintext credential storage with no masking on read.** All 5 OAuth client secrets / Apple private-key body round-trip in cleartext through the AJAX response. Browser DevTools network panel exposes them on every settings page load. **Worse than Task 18 (Printful), which masked `printful_secret_key` on response via `response_sanitize_callback`.**
  - **NEW: Sanitization-model correction.** Task 21's textarea-sanitizer claim ("`sanitize_text_field` collapses newlines") doesn't hold in the legacy AJAX path — `Settings::sanitize_options` is a strict no-op for any field without a registered callback. The collapse Task 21 measured must come from the new plugin-ui sanitizer (not the legacy one). This is a meaningful correction that may affect Tasks 20/21 P1 concerns.
- **API-key/token state in DB after task:** None — all sentinels used `__T_FAKE_KEY_SOCIAL_*` prefix. Grep `grep -E 'AC[a-f0-9]{32}|sk_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|Bearer\s+[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|-----BEGIN [A-Z ]+ KEY-----[^_]' _trace_artifacts/dokan_social_api_*.json` returned empty (the PEM header in `apple_key_content` is paired with a `__T_FAKE_KEY_*` body, no real key material). Option restored to absent state.
- **Commit SHA:** `6dbff6f33` (self-referential — amended once).
- **Concerns:**
  - **P0 — Tab is unmigrated.** No counterpart in `ProSettingsSchema`. Shipping the plugin-ui replacement without adding a `social-api-page` would silently strip the admin UI for Social Login configuration while leaving the runtime read sites intact (admins can no longer change keys; existing DB values keep working until someone tries to enable a new provider). Either: (a) add a new-schema page mirroring the 19 keys 1:1 (easy because of 100% key alignment), or (b) ship a feature-flag that keeps the legacy AJAX page mounted for `dokan_social_api` until migration lands.
  - **P0 — Plaintext OAuth secrets in HTTP response.** `fb_app_secret`, `twitter_app_secret`, `google_app_secret`, `linkedin_app_secret`, and `apple_key_content` (an entire RSA private key body) all return UNMASKED in every settings GET. Any compromised admin session, browser extension with DevTools access, or shared screen leaks all five OAuth client secrets. Recommend wiring a `response_sanitize_callback => '__return_blocked_sensitive_key'` (or the existing Printful-style wrapper) on these 5 fields, AND adding a "key is set but not shown" indicator in the UI.
  - **P0 — Save handler accepts unsanitized credential payloads.** Whitespace-only `google_app_id='   '` and HTML-injected `google_app_secret='<script>alert(2)</script>'` persisted verbatim. If a future renderer ever echoes these into a page without escaping (e.g. a debug screen, a connection-test modal), it becomes a stored-XSS sink. Register `sanitize_callback => 'sanitize_text_field'` on all 14 string keys.
  - **P1 — Cross-tab same-key names with `dokan_verification` (Task 16).** `apple_service_id`, `apple_team_id`, `apple_key_id`, `apple_key_content` exist in both storage rows. No current collision (different `wp_options` rows), but if the plugin-ui ever consolidates social/identity tabs into a single page+storage, the merge will silently overwrite one set. Namespace the fields (`social_apple_*` / `verification_apple_*`) at migration time.
  - **P1 — No URL validation on `*_redirect_url` / `apple_redirect_url` siblings.** The schema declares these as `type: 'html'` display-only — fine. But the AJAX endpoint will still accept and store any `settingsData[fb_redirect_url]` etc. if a client sends them (unknown-key persistence — confirmed via Save A). Adds DB noise but not exploitable; document the behavior.
  - **P2 — Empty-option default is `[]`.** Recurring Pro-tab pattern. The 5 `*_enable_status` fields rely on the read-site fallback `'on'` — fine in practice, but a fresh installation with `enabled='off'` will not surface defaults to the UI either.
  - **P2 — Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak persists.** Same as every prior Pro tab.
  - **P2 — Sanitization-model audit recommended.** Tasks 20 and 21 attribute behavior to `sanitize_text_field` that the legacy AJAX path does not produce (see "Sanitization model correction" above). Worth a one-pass review of those tabs' P1 concerns against the actual `Settings::sanitize_options` behavior (which is a no-op without registered callbacks).

### `dokan_shipping_status_setting` — Shipping Status (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_shipping_status_setting`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_shipping_status_setting` returned a 5-key payload (`shipping_status_list`, `shipping_status_provider`, `allow_mark_received='on'`, `enabled='on'`, `dashboard_menu_manager=[]`) — but `wp option get dokan_shipping_status_setting` returned `Could not get 'dokan_shipping_status_setting' option`. So the option row is **absent on disk**, and the rich `initial_get` payload is the **schema default injection** performed by `ShippingStatus::add_default_shipping_status()` (which writes `shipping_status_list` defaults via `update_option` the first time `get_option(..., [])` is read and is empty) PLUS the Pro MenuManager leak. To trace from a clean state, the pre-trace step ran `wp option delete dokan_shipping_status_setting` and captured the post-delete empty read to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_shipping_status_setting_empty_default.json` (`db: []`, `response: []`). **NEW vs Tasks 1–22:** This is the first Pro tab where the legacy code path **self-seeds the DB** on first read — distinct from the Tasks 12/16/18/21/22 pattern of "empty option → empty response, defaults live only in the schema."

**Source path:**
- Legacy schema: `dokan-pro/includes/Shipping/ShippingStatus.php:122-202`. Class `\WeDevs\DokanPro\Shipping\ShippingStatus` (core Pro feature, bootstrapped from Pro's main loader, not a module). Registers section `dokan_shipping_status_setting` via `dokan_settings_sections` filter at `:122`, and declares **4 persisted fields** via `dokan_settings_fields` at `:165`: `enabled` (switcher), `allow_mark_received` (switcher, `show_if dokan_shipping_status_setting.enabled === 'on'`), `shipping_status_provider` (multicheck, default = `dokan_get_shipping_tracking_default_providers_list()`, options = `dokan_get_shipping_tracking_providers_list()`), `shipping_status_list` (repeatable). Plus 0–2 transient warning fields (`digital_warning`, `wc_warning`) of `type: 'warning'` that are display-only (not persisted). **None of the 4 fields declares a `sanitize_callback` or `response_sanitize_callback`.**
- **Current-branch schema (plugin-ui flat array):** `dokan-pro/includes/Admin/Settings/Schema/ProSettingsSchema.php:1078-1227`. Declares page `id: 'shipment'`, `storage_key: 'dokan_settings_shipment'` (NOT `dokan_shipping_status_setting`), subpage `shipment-setting-page`, and these fields: `allows_shipment_tracking` (switch, `'on'`/`'off'`), `enable_shipstation_logging` (switch — Shipstation logging, not in legacy `dokan_shipping_status_setting` at all), `allow_mark_received` (switch, gated on `allows_shipment_tracking === 'on'`), one switch-per-provider (e.g. `sp-dhl`/`sp-fedex`/... — variant `switch`, enable_state value = the provider id), and `shipping_status_list` (variant `repeater`). **The new schema RENAMES `enabled` → `allows_shipment_tracking`, RESHAPES `shipping_status_provider` from a multicheck-encoded `{ 'sp-dhl': 'sp-dhl', ... }` map into N independent top-level switch fields, MOVES storage from `dokan_shipping_status_setting` to `dokan_settings_shipment`, and ADDS a new `enable_shipstation_logging` toggle.**
- **Read sites (Pro + Lite runtime), all reading `dokan_shipping_status_setting`:**
  - `dokan-pro/includes/Shipping/ShippingStatus.php:45` → `dokan_get_option('enabled', 'dokan_shipping_status_setting', 'off')` — master gate; if `!== 'on'`, the column-injection and admin-column callbacks short-circuit.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/Shipping/ShippingStatus.php:212,:250` → `get_option('dokan_shipping_status_setting', [])` for the default-seeder + write-back.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/Shipping/ShippingStatus.php:296` → `dokan_get_option('shipping_status_provider', 'dokan_shipping_status_setting')`.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/Shipping/ShippingStatus.php:297` → `dokan_get_option('shipping_status_list', 'dokan_shipping_status_setting')`.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/Shipping/Helper.php:24,:25` → `enabled`, `allow_mark_received`.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/functions.php:565` → `shipping_status_list`.
  - `dokan-pro/includes/REST/ShippingStatusController.php:125` → `get_option('dokan_shipping_status_setting', [])`.
  - `dokan-lite/includes/Assets.php:836,:1471`, `dokan-lite/includes/Dashboard/Templates/Orders.php:136` → `dokan_get_option('enabled', 'dokan_shipping_status_setting', 'off')` (cross-plugin reads — Lite reads a Pro-owned option). **Total read sites: 9 (7 Pro + 3 Lite), all 4 schema keys covered, 100% same-name alignment vs the legacy schema.**
- **Save handler:** `dokan-lite/includes/Admin/Settings.php save_settings_value()`. AJAX action `dokan_save_settings`, nonce `dokan_admin`. OVERWRITE via `update_option('dokan_shipping_status_setting', $value)`. `sanitize_options($value, 'edit')` runs but **none of the 4 fields declares a `sanitize_callback`** — pass-through.
- **AJAX session note:** Chrome tab `615167388` was unauthenticated at trace time (`document.cookie.includes('wordpress_logged_in') === false`, even after navigating to `/wp-admin/` to attempt session restore). **Fell back to `wp eval-file` driving `Settings::sanitize_options` (reflection on the private method) + `dokan_save_settings_value`/`dokan_get_settings_values` filter chains + `update_option`/`get_option` directly** — the same identical pipeline `Settings::save_settings_value()` and `Settings::get_settings_value()` run server-side. Round-trip semantics identical; only HTTP transport differs. Script: `/tmp/trace_shipping_status.php` (XDEBUG_MODE=off).
- **Filters/actions touching this option:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []` (Task 12 pattern).
  - **No `dokan_get_settings_values` filter on this option.** Read response is byte-identical to DB row.
  - **No `pre_update_option_dokan_shipping_status_setting` / `update_option_dokan_shipping_status_setting` listeners** found in either Pro or Lite (grepped).
  - `ShippingStatus::after_save_settings()` listens on `dokan_after_save_settings` (action, not a value filter) — fires `do_action('dokan_pro_register_shipping_status', $status['value'])` per row in `shipping_status_list`. **Triggered on Save A and Save B**; no DB mutation, no transient. Same pattern as `ShippingStatus::add_default_shipping_status()` does at first read. **Side effect: registers shipment statuses with the order-statuses system on every save** — repeated saves re-fire the action (idempotent for the receiver, but worth noting for any future listener).
- **Side effects on save:** `dokan_pro_register_shipping_status` action fires once per `shipping_status_list` row (5 fires on Save A, 2 on Save B). No `wp_posts`/`user_meta` mutation, no rewrite-rules touch, no transient flush.
