**Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip PASS.
- **Field count:** 0 keys in initial DB (option absent after pre-trace delete) → **16 keys after Save A** (4 known + 11 probes + 1 MenuManager leak) → **5 keys after Save B** (4 known + 1 MenuManager leak; OVERWRITE drops all 11 probes + unknown key). OVERWRITE confirmed.
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE, fully unfiltered.** Response byte-identical to DB (no `dokan_get_settings_values` listener for this option).
- **Auth approach:** **`wp eval-file` fallback** (browser session was unauthenticated; navigating to `/wp-admin/` did not restore the `wordpress_logged_in_*` cookie). Drives `Settings::sanitize_options` via reflection + the same filter chains used by the AJAX path. Same code path semantics as AJAX; only HTTP transport differs.
- **Filter findings:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Unchanged from Task 12 onward.
  - No `dokan_get_settings_values` transform on this option.
  - `sanitize_options` is a **NO-OP** for every shipping_status field (no callbacks registered) — consistent with the Task 22 correction.
  - `ShippingStatus::after_save_settings()` listens on `dokan_after_save_settings` action and re-fires `dokan_pro_register_shipping_status` once per `shipping_status_list` row.
- **Empty-option default:** `[]` on read (post-delete). But **on first read after a fresh install**, `ShippingStatus::add_default_shipping_status()` self-seeds `shipping_status_list` with 6 system rows via `update_option`. This means the option row **will reappear** on the next admin page load unless the seeder is also disabled. Worth noting for tests that assume "delete = stays deleted."
- **Pro MenuManager leak:** present in both saves.
- **Unknown-key persistence:** `unknown_shipping_field` + 10 boundary probes persisted in Save A, all dropped in Save B (OVERWRITE).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** Probes round-tripped UNMASKED. No production credential fields exist on this tab.
- **Schema↔read-site key skew audit:**
  - **Legacy schema (4 persisted fields) vs 9 read sites:** matched **4/4 (100% alignment)** on key names — every read site reads at the same name the schema declares (within storage `dokan_shipping_status_setting`). Matches Task 22's social_api 100% alignment.
  - **Current-branch plugin-ui schema vs legacy:** **shape-and-storage skew, not name-only skew.**
    - Storage row: legacy `dokan_shipping_status_setting` vs new `dokan_settings_shipment`. **Different `wp_options` row entirely.**
    - `enabled` → renamed to `allows_shipment_tracking`. **9 read sites still reference legacy `enabled` under legacy storage** — none updated.
    - `shipping_status_provider` → flattened from one multicheck-encoded assoc array into N top-level switch keys (`sp-dhl`, `sp-fedex`, ..., `sp-other`). Read sites still read at `shipping_status_provider` (e.g. `ShippingStatus.php:296`).
    - `enable_shipstation_logging` → **NEW key in new schema, no legacy counterpart on this tab.** Probably belongs to the ShipStation module — but the new schema puts it on the `shipment` page rather than within the shipstation module's own subtree. Worth verifying it doesn't double-up with whatever ShipStation already persists separately.
    - `allow_mark_received`, `shipping_status_list` — same names, just relocated.
    - Dead-key fraction in legacy vs new: 1/4 renamed (25%); shape change on 1/4 (25%); new schema adds 1 net new key; same-name 2/4 (50%).
  - **DB-only keys (declared by neither schema):** `dashboard_menu_manager` (Pro MenuManager leak — recurring).
- **Cross-tab collision audit:**
  - **No DB collision** with any prior tab — `dokan_shipping_status_setting` is a distinct `wp_options` row.
  - **Same-key-name overlap with Task 22's `dokan_social_api.enabled`, Task 16's `dokan_verification.enabled` (after the email-verification merge), Task 21's `dokan_email_verification.enabled`** — at minimum 4 traced Pro tabs use bare `enabled` as a master-gate key. **If any future flat-array consolidation merges multiple settings tabs into one storage row, `enabled` will N-way-collide.** Recommend prefixing on consolidation (`shipment_enabled` / `social_enabled` / `verification_enabled`).
  - **Same-key-name overlap on `allow_mark_received`** — also persisted by Task 16's `dokan_verification` schema in some branches; not currently a DB collision since they're separate rows, but the new schema does keep the same bare name on the `shipment-setting-page`.
  - **Cross-plugin read coupling:** Lite reads `dokan_shipping_status_setting.enabled` at three call sites (`Assets.php:836`, `:1471`, `Orders.php:136`). If the new schema migration ever ships **without** Pro present (Lite-only install where Pro's storage row never exists), those reads will silently return `'off'` from the default arg — fine. **But if Lite ever migrates to a flat-array of its own with a different storage key, the Pro read site for `enabled` won't follow.** Flagging because this is the first Pro tab traced where Lite reads a Pro-owned option.
- **Does new `ProSettingsSchema` declare this tab?** **Yes — but renamed/reshaped/relocated.** Page `id: 'shipment'`, `storage_key: 'dokan_settings_shipment'`. **Migration is non-trivial: requires (a) renaming `enabled` → `allows_shipment_tracking`, (b) flattening the `shipping_status_provider` assoc array into N top-level switches, (c) updating 9 read sites (7 in Pro, 3 cross-plugin in Lite), (d) deciding whether `enable_shipstation_logging` shares this storage row or belongs elsewhere.** Of the 22 Pro tabs traced so far, this is the **most structurally-divergent** new-schema counterpart traced (Task 21 relocated a tab; Task 22 didn't migrate at all; this one migrates but reshapes the data model).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** No credential fields on this tab.
- **Round-trip:** `_trace_artifacts/dokan_shipping_status_setting_reload.json` byte-identical to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_shipping_status_setting_after_B.json` `.db` slice (PHP `==` confirmed by trace script: `ROUND_TRIP=PASS`).
- **DB state restored:** `wp option delete dokan_shipping_status_setting` executed by the trace script at the end (`FINAL_STATE=ABSENT`). Caveat: the next admin page load that hits `ShippingStatus::add_default_shipping_status()` will re-seed the option with 6 default `shipping_status_list` rows — this is by design, not a leak.
- **Surprises (NEW vs Tasks 1–22):**
  - **NEW: Pro tab self-seeds its option row on first read.** `ShippingStatus::add_default_shipping_status()` (called on `dokan_pro_register_shipping_status` action — fires from `ShippingStatus::__construct()`) writes 6 default `shipping_status_list` rows into `wp_options` via `update_option` if the option is empty. **First Pro tab traced where the option will reappear in the DB even after `wp option delete`.** All prior Pro tabs read `[]` and stayed at `[]` unless the admin explicitly saves. Has implications for migration: a migrator running before a fresh admin page load will see an empty option; running after the seeder will see 6 rows. **Migrator MUST handle both states.**
  - **NEW: First "schema-RESHAPE" migration in the new plugin-ui schema.** Tasks 1–22 found "schema absent" (Task 22) or "schema present but relocated" (Task 21) — this is the first tab where the new schema KEEPS the field set conceptually but RESHAPES the storage model (multicheck-encoded assoc array → N independent fields). Round-trip behavior of the legacy AJAX path is unaffected; the migration shim must reshape.
  - **NEW: First "key-rename" migration.** `enabled` → `allows_shipment_tracking` is the first traced rename of a top-level setting key. **9 read sites still reference `enabled`** — migration is incomplete on the read side.
  - **NEW: First cross-plugin read of a Pro-owned option from Lite.** `Lite Assets.php:836,:1471` and `Lite Orders.php:136` read `dokan_get_option('enabled', 'dokan_shipping_status_setting', 'off')`. Migration of this Pro tab without coordinated updates to Lite's read sites will silently regress shipment-tracking detection on the storefront.
  - **NEW: Schema-extension on new side.** `enable_shipstation_logging` exists in the new `dokan_settings_shipment` schema but has no legacy counterpart in `dokan_shipping_status_setting`. Either it's a feature add or it has been moved here from a ShipStation-module-specific schema (probably the latter — `dokan-pro/modules/shipstation/` exists). Either way, the legacy AJAX path **does not** accept or emit this key.
  - **NEW: `dokan_after_save_settings` action re-fires `dokan_pro_register_shipping_status` per row.** Confirmed not a side effect on DB (no extra `update_option`), but any third-party listener that does cleanup on this action will run on every settings save — worth surfacing for plugin authors.
  - **NOT new:** sanitize_options no-op, MenuManager leak, unknown-key persistence, OVERWRITE save, plaintext storage — all consistent with Tasks 12–22.
- **API-key/token state in DB after task:** None — all sentinels prefixed `__T_FAKE_*`. Final state: option deleted (`FINAL_STATE=ABSENT`); will re-seed `shipping_status_list` defaults on next admin page load (by design).
- **Commit SHA:** `e00ed6155` (self-referential — amended once for SHA backfill).
- **Concerns:**
  - **P0 — `enabled` → `allows_shipment_tracking` rename + storage-key change leaves 9 read sites stale.** Without coordinated read-site updates, the new admin UI will write to `dokan_settings_shipment.allows_shipment_tracking` while every consumer continues to read `dokan_shipping_status_setting.enabled` and find `'off'`. **Result: admins flip the toggle on, the storefront shipment-tracking column never lights up.** Mitigation: either (a) ship a one-way migration that writes both keys for a transition window, or (b) update all 9 read sites to the new name + storage in the same PR that flips the schema.
  - **P0 — `shipping_status_provider` shape change.** Legacy stores `{ 'sp-dhl': 'sp-dhl', 'sp-fedex': 'sp-fedex', ... }` under one key; new schema stores `sp-dhl: 'sp-dhl'`, `sp-fedex: 'sp-fedex'`, ... as N top-level keys. **The Pro read at `ShippingStatus.php:296` (`dokan_get_option('shipping_status_provider', ...)`) will return `null`/empty on the new storage.** A migrator MUST collect the N top-level switch values back into an assoc array under `shipping_status_provider` at read time (or update the read site).
  - **P0 — Cross-plugin read coupling.** `dokan-lite/includes/Assets.php:836`, `:1471`, `Dashboard/Templates/Orders.php:136` read a **Pro-owned option** from Lite. The new schema decouples them (storage in `dokan_settings_shipment` is Pro-only). **Lite needs to be aware of the new storage key OR Pro needs to keep writing the legacy `enabled` key for backward compatibility.**
  - **P1 — Schema-add of `enable_shipstation_logging`.** Has no legacy counterpart in `dokan_shipping_status_setting`. Audit whether this duplicates a ShipStation-module-specific option (likely a separate `wp_options` row owned by the shipstation module); if so, the new schema's flat-array model now stores it twice, OR has migrated it without removing the old row. Worth a quick check before shipping.
  - **P1 — Self-seeding default writer means `wp option delete` is non-permanent.** `ShippingStatus::add_default_shipping_status()` re-seeds on next admin page load. The trace-restore step is misleading: option is gone NOW but will reappear at the next request. Document this for any flat-array migrator that runs `wp option delete` to ensure idempotency.
  - **P1 — `dokan_after_save_settings` re-fires `dokan_pro_register_shipping_status` per row on every save.** Idempotent today, but any listener doing non-idempotent work (cache flush, transient invalidation, status registration with side effects) will run N times per save. Document the contract for module authors.
  - **P1 — No per-row sanitization of `shipping_status_list`.** `<script>`/`javascript:`/5000-char/`\n`+`\r` in a row's `value` all persist. The status `value` is rendered into vendor-facing dropdowns and order column cells via `dokan_get_template_part('orders/shipment/html-shipping-status', ...)` — confirm template uses `esc_html()`/`esc_attr()` before declaring this safe. Recommend a `sanitize_callback` on the field that maps over the array and runs `sanitize_text_field` per row's `value`/`desc`, and `sanitize_key` on `id`.
  - **P1 — Unknown-key persistence + no URL/length validation.** Recurring pattern (Tasks 16, 18, 21, 22). Adds DB noise; the `probe_long` 5000-char payload persists verbatim. No new finding, but worth re-flagging.
  - **P2 — `enabled` is a bare key name colliding with master-gates on Tasks 16, 21, 22, and now 23.** Four Pro tabs use the same bare key name in their respective storage rows. Any future single-storage-row consolidation will collide. Recommend prefixing on consolidation.
  - **P2 — Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak persists.** Recurring.

### `dokan_quote_settings` — Quote Settings (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_quote_settings`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_quote_settings` returned `[]` (empty array). `wp option get dokan_quote_settings` confirmed `Could not get 'dokan_quote_settings' option` — option row **absent on disk**. Unlike Task 23 (`dokan_shipping_status_setting`), **no self-seeder writes defaults on first read**; the schema-declared defaults (`enable_out_of_stock='on'`, `enable_ajax_add_to_quote='on'`, others `'off'`/`0`) live only in the schema and are supplied per-read by `dokan_get_option`'s third-argument default. Captured `_trace_artifacts/dokan_quote_settings_empty_default.json` (`db: []`, `response: []`). Pattern matches Tasks 12/16/18/21/22 ("empty option → empty response, defaults schema-only").

**Source path:**
- Legacy schema: `dokan-pro/modules/request-for-quotation/includes/Admin/Settings.php:35-118`. Class `\WeDevs\DokanPro\Modules\RequestForQuotation\Admin\Settings`, registered when the `request-for-quotation` Pro module is loaded. **Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip PASS.
- **Field count:** 0 keys in initial DB (option absent) → **19 keys after Save A** (6 known + 12 probes + 1 MenuManager leak) → **7 keys after Save B** (6 known + 1 MenuManager leak; OVERWRITE drops all 12 probes + unknown key). OVERWRITE confirmed.
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE, fully unfiltered.** Response byte-identical to DB (no `dokan_get_settings_values` listener for this option).
- **Auth approach:** **`wp eval-file` fallback** (Chrome session unauthenticated, continuing from Tasks 22/23). Drives `Settings::sanitize_options` via reflection + the same filter chains used by the AJAX path. Same code path semantics as AJAX; only HTTP transport differs.
- **Filter findings:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Unchanged from Task 12 onward.
  - No `dokan_get_settings_values` transform on this option.
  - `sanitize_options` is a **NO-OP** for every quote field (no callbacks registered) — consistent with Tasks 22/23.
  - **No `dokan_after_save_settings` module listener** for this tab. No side effects on save.
  - **First numeric field traced where validation lives at read time, not save time:** `SettingsHelper.php:48` wraps with `(float) ( -1 * abs(...) )`, so save accepts anything but reads always return `<= 0`. Distinct from Tasks 1-23 where validation (when present) lived in `sanitize_callback`.
- **Empty-option default:** `[]` on read. **No self-seeder** writes defaults on first read (distinct from Task 23). Schema defaults supplied only by `dokan_get_option`'s third-argument default at each read site.
- **Pro MenuManager leak:** present in both saves.
- **Unknown-key persistence:** `unknown_quote_field` + 11 boundary probes persisted in Save A, all dropped in Save B (OVERWRITE).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** Probes round-tripped UNMASKED. No production credential fields exist on this tab.
- **Schema↔read-site key skew audit:**
  - **Legacy schema (6 persisted fields) vs 6 read sites:** matched **6/6 (100% alignment)** on key names — every read site reads at the same name the schema declares (within storage `dokan_quote_settings`). Matches Task 22 (social_api) and Task 23 (shipping_status) on the legacy side. **All read sites in a single helper class (`SettingsHelper`)** — tightest read-site cohesion seen so far across the 24 traced tabs.
  - **Current-branch plugin-ui schema vs legacy:** **N/A — `ProSettingsSchema` does not declare a `dokan_quote_settings` (or "quote") tab.** No migration target exists on this branch. **This is the first Pro tab traced that has NO new-schema counterpart at all.** Likely deliberate (module-owned tabs may follow a different migration plan, since the module's `Admin\Settings` class registers via the legacy `dokan_settings_sections`/`dokan_settings_fields` filters and the new plugin-ui flat-array doesn't currently honor those module-side filters).
  - **DB-only keys (declared by neither schema):** `dashboard_menu_manager` (Pro MenuManager leak — recurring).
- **Cross-tab collision audit:**
  - **No DB collision** with any prior tab — `dokan_quote_settings` is a distinct `wp_options` row.
  - **No bare `enabled` key on this tab** (unlike Tasks 16, 21, 22, 23). Field names are all prefixed (`enable_out_of_stock`, `enable_ajax_add_to_quote`, etc.) — **best-named tab seen so far for future flat-array consolidation.** If everything ever merges to one storage row, these 6 keys would not collide with any of the master-gate `enabled` keys from prior tabs.
  - **No cross-plugin read coupling:** Lite does not read this option. All 6 reads originate in the Pro module that owns the schema.
- **Does new `ProSettingsSchema` declare this tab?** **No.** Page does not exist in `ProSettingsSchema.php`. **Y/N: N. Shape: n/a — no migration target on this branch.** First Pro tab traced (of 24) with no new-schema counterpart.
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** No credential fields on this tab.
- **Round-trip:** `_trace_artifacts/dokan_quote_settings_reload.json` byte-identical to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_quote_settings_after_B.json` `.db` slice (PHP `==` confirmed by trace script: `ROUND_TRIP=PASS`).
- **DB state restored:** `delete_option('dokan_quote_settings')` executed by the trace script at the end (`FINAL_STATE=ABSENT`). No self-seeder will re-create it (distinct from Task 23).

**Surprises:**
- **NEW: First Pro tab traced with NO new-schema counterpart.** `ProSettingsSchema` does not declare a "quote" page or `dokan_quote_settings` storage key. Every Pro tab in Tasks 1-23 had at minimum a relocated counterpart; this one has none. **Implication:** module-owned (`modules/*`) tabs may be on a deferred migration path, or the plan is for module tabs to keep using the legacy `dokan_settings_sections`/`dokan_settings_fields` filter pipeline indefinitely even after the rest of the schema flips to flat-array. **Action needed:** confirm migration plan for the 22+ module-owned tabs.
- **NEW: First field with read-time-only normalization.** `decrease_offered_price` is stored as user-entered string (e.g. `'25'`), but every read at `SettingsHelper.php:48` returns `(float) ( -1 * abs( (float) ... ) ) = -25.0`. Save handler doesn't know this transform exists. **Risk:** if anyone migrates this field via a one-shot `update_option` that bypasses the read helper, downstream code that expects negative floats will get positive ones. The normalization belongs in a `sanitize_callback` (save-time) or as an explicit `response_sanitize_callback` (read-time visible in API), not buried in a helper.
- **NEW: Tightest read-site cohesion of any traced tab.** All 6 read sites live in a single 70-line helper class (`SettingsHelper.php`). Refactor target: trivial. If this tab is migrated, a single file edit covers every read site.
- **NOT new:** sanitize_options no-op, MenuManager leak, unknown-key persistence, OVERWRITE save, plaintext storage, no `pre_update_option_*`/`update_option_*` listeners — all consistent with Tasks 12-23.

**API-key/token state in DB after task:** None — all sentinels prefixed `__T_FAKE_*` / `probe_*`. Final state: option deleted (`FINAL_STATE=ABSENT`). No self-seeder, so it stays absent until the admin saves the quote tab again.

**Commit SHA:** `eca1641b8` (self-referential — amended once for SHA backfill).

**Concerns:**
- **P0 — No new-schema counterpart.** `dokan_quote_settings` is not in `ProSettingsSchema.php`. **The new plugin-ui settings page cannot render this tab.** If the legacy page is removed/disabled before module tabs are migrated, admins lose access to 6 active feature toggles (out-of-stock quotes, ajax add-to-quote, redirect, decrease-offered-price percent, convert-to-order, converter-display). **Mitigation options:** (a) keep the legacy `Settings` page mounted alongside the new flat-array page until all module tabs migrate, (b) extend the new schema to honor `dokan_settings_sections`/`dokan_settings_fields` filters as a fallback for unmigrated tabs, (c) migrate this and the other 21+ module tabs into `ProSettingsSchema` before flipping. **This concern likely applies to most module-owned tabs** (request-for-quotation, vendor-staff, table-rate-shipping, distance-rate-shipping, vendor-subscription, etc.) — recommend a separate audit pass that confirms which `modules/*` tabs are in `ProSettingsSchema` and which are not.
- **P1 — Read-time normalization for `decrease_offered_price`.** The `(float) ( -1 * abs(...) )` wrapper at the read site is invisible to anything reading the raw DB row (REST APIs, migration scripts, debug tools). Move into either (a) a save-time `sanitize_callback` so the DB stores the normalized form, or (b) a `response_sanitize_callback` so the AJAX response and any future REST endpoint return the normalized form. **As written, save-stored value and runtime-consumed value differ — silent corruption surface for any migrator that round-trips through DB.**
- **P1 — Unknown-key persistence + no per-field sanitization.** Recurring (Tasks 16, 21, 22, 23). `<script>` tags, `javascript:` URLs, 5000-char strings, whitespace-only values, negative-number strings at numeric fields — all persist verbatim. No new finding, but worth re-flagging given the lack of any `sanitize_callback` across the entire tab.
- **P2 — Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak persists.** Recurring.
- **P2 — Module activation coupling.** All 6 fields exist only if the `request-for-quotation` Pro module is active. If a site has the option row populated (from prior module activation) and the module is later disabled, the option row remains as orphan data. No GC. (Same pattern likely true for every module-owned tab — not specific to this one but first explicit observation.)

  - **`dokan_after_saving_settings` listener at Admin.php:18** — iterates `$option_value['rma_reasons']` and fires `dokan_pro_register_rms_reason` (an action, not a filter) **once per reason row**. Confirmed in trace: with 3 reasons in Save A, the action fired exactly 3 times. **NEW: First settings tab whose save handler fires a per-row action**, allowing 3rd-party plugins to react to each newly-registered reason (e.g., for cataloging or audit logging). No core consumer registers for `dokan_pro_register_rms_reason` (grep returns the do_action site only). PHP **warning surface**: `Admin::after_save_settings` does **NOT** guard against `rma_reasons` being missing or non-array — `foreach ( $option_value['rma_reasons'] as ... )` would emit a Warning if the save payload omits `rma_reasons` or sends `null`. (PHP 8+ would throw a TypeError if `rma_reasons` is not iterable.)
- **Side effects on save:** Per-reason action fires (described above). **No transient flush, no rewrite-rules touch, no `wp_posts`/`user_meta` mutation, no cron scheduling, no DB-table mutation** observed in the trace.

**Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip PASS.
- **Field count:** 6 keys in initial DB (5 known + `dashboard_menu_manager`) → **18 keys after Save A** (5 known + 12 probes + 1 MenuManager leak) → **6 keys after Save B** (5 known + 1 MenuManager leak; OVERWRITE drops all 12 probes + unknown key). OVERWRITE confirmed.
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE, fully unfiltered.** Response byte-identical to DB after the `sanitize_options('read')` no-op pass and the `dokan_get_settings_values` filter (which has no listener for this option).
- **Auth approach:** **`wp eval-file` fallback via host WP install** (`/Users/mahbub/Development/Projects/core-dokan`). Chrome session unauthenticated since Task 22; wp-env doesn't have Pro installed. Trace drives `Settings::sanitize_options` via reflection + the same filter/action chain the AJAX handler runs, **and force-instantiates `RMA\Admin`** to register the per-reason action listener (since WP-CLI is not `is_admin()`).
- **Filter findings:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Unchanged from Task 12 onward.
  - No `dokan_get_settings_values` transform on this option.
  - `sanitize_options` is a **NO-OP** for every RMA field (no callbacks registered) — consistent with Tasks 22/23/24.
  - **`dokan_after_saving_settings` Pro listener at `Admin.php:18`** — fires `dokan_pro_register_rms_reason` per `rma_reasons` row. **Confirmed: 3 reasons → action fires 3 times.** No de-dup, no idempotency, no validation. **First settings tab traced where save fires a per-row action.**
  - **Hook spelling: `dokan_after_saving_settings`** (gerund) not `dokan_after_save_settings`. Initial trace draft used the wrong name and reported 0 fires — fixed and verified.
- **Empty-option default:** `[]` on read (after `delete_option`). **No self-seeder** writes defaults on first read (distinct from Task 23). The pre-existing 3 `rma_reasons` rows in the live DB were placed by a prior admin save, not by any installer/upgrader code.
- **Pro MenuManager leak:** present in both saves.
- **Unknown-key persistence:** `unknown_rma_field` + 11 boundary probes persisted in Save A, all dropped in Save B (OVERWRITE).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** Probes round-tripped UNMASKED. No production credential fields exist on this tab.
- **Schema↔read-site key skew audit:**
  - **Legacy schema (5 persisted fields) vs 11 read sites:** matched **5/5 (100% alignment)** on key names. Every read site reads at the same legacy-schema name. **No DB-only key reads.**
  - **Current-branch plugin-ui schema vs legacy:** **`RmaSettingsSchema` exists but is NOT registered** (no `new RmaSettingsSchema()` / `->register()` call anywhere). **Key skew if it ever ships as-is:**
    - `rma_enable_refund_request` → `rma_refund_requests` (RENAME — 5 read sites break).
    - `rma_enable_coupon_request` → `rma_coupon_requests` (RENAME — 3 read sites break).
    - `rma_policy` → `rma_refund_policy` (RENAME — 2 read sites + Elementor widget break).
    - `rma_order_status`: name preserved but **value shape changes** (`wc-completed` → `completed`) — 2 read sites use `'wc-completed'` fallback explicitly and will mismatch on the new value space.
    - `rma_reasons`: name preserved but **row shape changes** (`{id, value}` → `{id, title, order}`) — `BlockData.php:84` reads `$rma['reasons']` (note: NOT `value`) and `functions.php:77` iterates rows assuming `value` key; both break post-migration.
    - Default flip: refund + coupon both flip from `'off'` (legacy) to `'on'` (new) — **semantic regression risk** for sites that upgrade with no prior save (new install starts with both ON instead of OFF).
  - **DB-only keys (declared by neither schema):** `dashboard_menu_manager` (Pro MenuManager leak — recurring).
- **Cross-tab collision audit:**
  - **No DB collision** with any prior tab — `dokan_rma` is a distinct `wp_options` row.
  - **No bare `enabled` key** on this tab — every field is `rma_*` prefixed. **Safe for future flat-array merge** (similar to Task 24's quote settings). However, the new schema introduces `rma_refund_requests` / `rma_coupon_requests` which collide with **nothing today**, but the **shorter names increase collision risk** if anyone ever adds a non-RMA `refund_requests` field at the global level.
  - **Cross-plugin reads:** Lite does NOT read `dokan_rma`. **Cross-module read:** `dokan-pro/modules/elementor/includes/Widgets/ProductRMA.php:170` reads `rma_policy` from the RMA module — first cross-module read coupling traced on a Pro tab (Task 24's quote-settings reads stayed inside its own module). **If the Elementor module is active but the RMA module is disabled, this read still works (returns `''` default) but the Elementor widget exposes a refund policy form for a module that doesn't accept warranty requests.**
- **Does new `ProSettingsSchema` declare this tab?** **N — `ProSettingsSchema.php` itself does not declare `rma`.** **However** the module ships `RmaSettingsSchema.php` (sibling to other migrated module schemas) that **would** append it under `subpage_id: 'rma' → page_id: 'moderation'`, **but the class is never instantiated**. **Y/N: N as wired today. Y as authored but orphaned.** This is **distinct from Task 24** (quote-settings has no schema class at all). **NEW: First Pro tab traced with a defined-but-unwired new-schema class.**
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** No credential fields on this tab.
- **Round-trip:** `_trace_artifacts/dokan_rma_reload.json` byte-identical to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_rma_after_B.json` `.db` slice (PHP `==` confirmed by trace script: `ROUND_TRIP=PASS`).
- **DB state restored:** trace script restored the pre-existing 6-key option row via `update_option('dokan_rma', $starting_db)` at the end (`FINAL_STATE=RESTORED`). DB live state matches `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json` after the trace.

**Surprises:**
- **NEW: First Pro tab with a defined-but-not-wired new-schema class.** `RmaSettingsSchema.php` declares the new field layout, but no module bootstrap instantiates it. Distinct from Task 24 (no schema at all) and from Tasks 1-23 (schema exists and is wired). **Implication:** when someone wires it (likely a single-line change in `modules/rma/module.php::initiate()`), it lands with the key-rename + row-shape changes documented above unless migration code lands first.
- **NEW: First Pro tab whose new schema RENAMES persisted keys.** `rma_enable_refund_request` → `rma_refund_requests` (and 2 others). Every Pro tab in Tasks 1-23/24 kept key names identical across legacy and new schemas (or had no new schema). **Migration must alias or rewrite the wp_options row** before flipping; otherwise 10+ read sites silently read defaults instead of saved values.
- **NEW: First Pro tab where the schema default conflicts with the schema's own options list.** `rma_order_status` defaults to `'seller'`, which is not in `wc_get_order_statuses()` (the field's own option source). Every read site overrides with `'wc-completed'` at the call site, hiding the bug. If the new schema is wired and writes the legacy default into a fresh option row, the rendered select would show no selection (no matching option).
- **NEW: First settings tab whose save handler fires a per-row action.** `dokan_pro_register_rms_reason` fires once per `rma_reasons` row in Save A. No de-dup; no listeners in core. **Risk:** if a future listener is added and the admin re-saves with the same N reasons, it fires N times again (idempotency burden falls on listeners).
- **NEW: First numeric-default → semantic flip in the new schema.** `rma_refund_requests` and `rma_coupon_requests` default `'on'` in new schema vs `'off'` in legacy schema. Fresh installs on the new flat-array schema will silently enable refund + coupon request UIs by default — opposite of legacy behavior.
- **NEW: First cross-module read coupling on a Pro tab.** `modules/elementor` reads `rma_policy` from `modules/rma`. Tasks 1-24 read sites stayed inside the module that owns the schema.
- **NEW: First repeatable-field tab + first per-row payload validation gap.** `rma_reasons` rows are stored verbatim — `<script>` tags in row `value` fields persist unfiltered.
- **NEW: First missing-`is_admin()`-guard trace gotcha.** The legacy Admin class is gated on `is_admin()` at `module.php:93`, so WP-CLI does NOT auto-instantiate it. Trace script had to force-instantiate to observe the per-reason action — otherwise the per-row action would appear absent. **Methodology note for the remaining 6 module-owned tabs:** check each module's `initiate()` for `is_admin()` gating before relying on side-effect observability via `wp eval-file`.
- **NOT new:** sanitize_options no-op, MenuManager leak, unknown-key persistence, OVERWRITE save, plaintext storage, no `pre_update_option_*`/`update_option_*` listeners — all consistent with Tasks 12-24.

**API-key/token state in DB after task:** None — all sentinels prefixed `__T_FAKE_*` / `probe_*`. Final state: **option row restored** to the pre-trace 6-key snapshot (`FINAL_STATE=RESTORED`).

**Commit SHA:** `f5a701cf9` (self-referential; will be amended once at end of task chain if needed).

**Concerns:**
- **P0 — Key renames in new schema with no migration code.** `rma_enable_refund_request → rma_refund_requests`, `rma_enable_coupon_request → rma_coupon_requests`, `rma_policy → rma_refund_policy`. **10 read sites** (5 + 3 + 2) reference the legacy names. If `RmaSettingsSchema` is wired without a one-shot upgrader that renames keys in the live `dokan_rma` row, sites lose refund/coupon toggles + refund policy after upgrade — silently fall back to defaults. **Mitigation:** ship an upgrader in `dokan-pro/includes/Upgrade/Upgraders/V_X_Y_0.php` that copies old→new keys, OR update read sites to alias both names, OR keep legacy names in the new schema.
- **P0 — Row-shape change for `rma_reasons` with no migration code.** Legacy `{id, value}` rows vs new `{id, title, order}` rows. `dokan-pro/modules/rma/includes/functions.php:77` + `BlockData.php:84` iterate assuming `value` key; both break post-migration. Plus `BlockData.php:84` reads `$rma['reasons']` (note: NOT `rma_reasons`) — typo or legacy alias surface that needs cross-check.
- **P0 — `rma_order_status` value-space change.** Legacy `wc-completed` vs new `completed` (no `wc-` prefix). `Order.php:211` + `Hooks.php:24` fall back to `'wc-completed'`; consuming code passes this directly to `wc_get_order_status_name()`-style functions that may or may not accept the unprefixed form.
- **P1 — Default flip from `off` to `on` for refund + coupon switches.** Fresh installs on the new schema enable both flows by default. **Semantic regression** for any admin who relied on legacy default-off behavior.
- **P1 — `RmaSettingsSchema` is defined but not wired.** Whoever lands the wiring change inherits all P0 concerns above unless migration code lands in the same PR.
- **P1 — Stored XSS surface on `rma_policy` + `rma_reasons[].value`.** No `wp_kses_post` / `sanitize_text_field` on save. `<script>` tags persisted verbatim. Stored XSS depends on whether render sites escape — needs render-site audit (`echo $rma_policy` vs `echo wp_kses_post($rma_policy)`).
- **P1 — Schema default `'seller'` for `rma_order_status` is invalid.** Not in own options list. Bug masked only because every read site supplies a different fallback. If anyone calls `get_option('dokan_rma')['rma_order_status']` directly (without the helper), they get `'seller'` which matches no order status.
- **P1 — `Admin::after_save_settings` lacks input guard.** `foreach ( $option_value['rma_reasons'] as ... )` will warn if `rma_reasons` is missing or non-iterable. Save B in this trace included `rma_reasons` so didn't trip it, but a UI that sends no reasons would.
- **P2 — Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak persists.** Recurring.
- **P2 — Module activation coupling.** If RMA module is disabled, the `dokan_rma` row remains as orphan. No GC.
- **P2 — Cross-module coupling.** `modules/elementor` reads `rma_policy`. If RMA is disabled, the Elementor widget renders an empty policy area without warning. Either the widget should also depend on RMA being active, or it should hide the policy block when empty.
- **P2 — `dokan_pro_register_rms_reason` is fire-and-forget.** No documented contract for listeners. Fires per-row on every save. Adding listeners requires idempotency.

### `dokan_live_chat` — Live Chat (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_live_chat`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_live_chat` returned 10 keys (`enable='on'`, `provider='messenger'`, `theme_color='#0084FF'`, `app_id=''`, `app_secret=''`, `wa_opening_method='in_app'`, `wa_pre_filled_message='Hello {store_name}, I have an enquiry regarding your store at {store_url}'`, `chat_button_seller_page=''`, `chat_button_product_page='above_tab'`, `dashboard_menu_manager=[]`). `wp option get dokan_live_chat --format=json` on the host install returned the same 10 keys. **Notable:** the live `provider='messenger'` value is **NOT in the legacy schema's option list** anymore — the legacy schema only declares `talkjs|tawkto|whatsapp` (a comment at `AdminSettings.php:80` says `// TODO: Remove all the associated code for fb messenger`). The stored value is unselectable in the current UI but still survives because save is unfiltered. **Also:** `theme_color='#0084FF'` is an **orphan** — no schema field writes it, but `AdminSettings::get_theme_color()` + `Messenger.php:39` still read it. Captured `_trace_artifacts/dokan_live_chat_empty_default.json` (`db: []`, `response: []` — after `delete_option`). Pattern matches Tasks 12/16/18/21/22/24/25 ("empty option → empty response").

**Source path:**
- Legacy schema: `dokan-pro/modules/live-chat/includes/AdminSettings.php:63-158`. Class `\WeDevs\DokanPro\Modules\LiveChat\AdminSettings`, instantiated unconditionally at `module.php:51` (`$this->container['admin_settings'] = new AdminSettings();` — **NOT gated on `is_admin()`** unlike Task 25's RMA module). Adds section `dokan_live_chat` via `dokan_settings_sections` filter at `:27` (priority 20), declares fields via `dokan_settings_fields` at `:28` (priority 20). **No `dokan_after_saving_settings` listener** on this module. **8 persisted fields:** `enable` (switcher, default `'on'`), `provider` (radio, default `'talkjs'`, options `talkjs|tawkto|whatsapp` — `messenger` is **removed**), `app_id` (text, `secret_text: true`), `app_secret` (text, `secret_text: true`), `wa_opening_method` (select, default `'in_app'`, options `in_browser|in_app`), `wa_pre_filled_message` (textarea, default `'Hello {store_name}, I have an enquiry regarding your store at {store_url}'`), `chat_button_seller_page` (switcher, no default), `chat_button_product_page` (select, default `'above_tab'`, options `above_tab|inside_tab|dont_show`). **None of the 8 fields declares a `sanitize_callback` or `response_sanitize_callback`** — including the two credential-bearing fields with `secret_text: true` (the `secret_text` flag is **UI-only** — input type=password — and does NOT trigger any server-side masking on save or read).

  - `provider` → `livechat_provider` (RENAME) — **adds `messenger` BACK** to options list (`messenger|talkjs|tawkto|whatsapp`). Reverses legacy schema's removal.

The live-DB `provider='messenger'` came from an older version of the schema that included `messenger` as a provider option — i.e., it was a legitimate saved value at the time, and the schema-shrink left it as an unselectable-but-readable orphan value.

The GET response returns plaintext credentials. **NEW: First Pro tab traced with declared credential fields where no server-side masking exists.** (Tasks 11-13 had no credentials; Tasks 14-19 either had no credentials or did not test masking; Task 22 social_api had OAuth secrets — re-check whether THAT tab masked; Task 23 verification_sms_gateways had API keys — same question. To be cross-referenced in the final-pass audit.)

  - **Current-branch plugin-ui schema vs legacy:** `LiveChatSettingsSchema` is **fully wired** (registered in `module.php:19`). **Key skew if it ships as-is:**
    - 6 RENAMES: `enable→livechat_enabled`, `provider→livechat_provider`, `app_id→livechat_app_id`, `app_secret→livechat_app_secret`, `chat_button_seller_page→livechat_vendor_page_button`, `chat_button_product_page→livechat_product_page_button`. **All 13 read sites still reference legacy names** — will silently fall back to defaults after migration.
    - 2 DROPS: `wa_opening_method`, `wa_pre_filled_message`. `Whatsapp.php:246`/`:267` will read `null` post-migration (no default supplied at the call site).

Distinct DB row keeps it safe today, but **future flat-array merge would collide** with any other tab that surfaces a bare `enable`. New schema renames to `livechat_enabled` which fixes this.

If LiveChat module is disabled but Elementor module is active, the Elementor tag/widget renders without the store chat button — but the widget itself still appears, presenting a UI dead-end.

Appends `subpage_id: 'livechat' → page_id: 'moderation'`. **This is distinct from Task 24 (no class), Task 25 (defined-but-unwired), and matches the pattern of every other migrated module schema (subscription, geolocation, delivery-time).** **NEW vs Task 25:** first tab in the audit where the new schema is **fully wired AND substantially diverges from legacy** (6 renames + 2 drops + 1 default flip + 1 option-list expansion).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** `app_id` + `app_secret` returned **UNMASKED** in the response despite carrying `secret_text: true` in legacy and `variant: 'show_hide'` in new schema. The flags are UI affordances, not server-side redaction.

**Surprises:**
- **NEW: First Pro tab traced where the new schema is fully wired AND substantially diverges from the legacy schema.** 6 RENAMES + 2 DROPS + 1 default flip + 1 option-list expansion. Task 24 had no new schema; Task 25 had an unwired schema; all earlier migrated module schemas kept legacy key names. This is the first one that lands the rename storm in active wiring.
- **NEW: First Pro tab where a live DB key has no schema authority in either legacy or new schema, yet a Pro read site actively consumes it.** `theme_color='#0084FF'` is read by `Messenger.php:39` + `AdminSettings::get_theme_color()`. Pure orphan with active read coupling. The legacy schema removed `messenger` from the provider list (TODO at `:80` to delete all messenger code), but the messenger render path + theme_color read survived the partial cleanup.
- **NEW: First Pro tab whose new schema RE-ADDS an option-list value that the legacy schema removed.** `provider='messenger'` was removed from legacy options (with a deletion TODO) but is added back in `LiveChatSettingsSchema`. Existing DB rows with `provider='messenger'` (including the live install) become valid again under the new schema. Either the new schema's intent is to keep messenger or it's a copy-paste oversight that re-introduces dead code.
- **NEW: First Pro tab traced with declared credential fields where no server-side masking exists.** `app_id` + `app_secret` use `secret_text: true` (legacy) / `variant: 'show_hide'` (new) — both are UI-only. The GET response returns plaintext credentials to anyone with `manage_options`. (Re-audit Tasks 22 social_api, 23 verification_sms_gateways in final pass to confirm whether their OAuth secrets / API keys are similarly leaked.)
- **NEW: First Pro tab traced with a bare unprefixed `enable` key in the legacy schema.** Every other tab uses prefixed booleans. Safe in the current per-tab option-row scheme; **dangerous in any future flat-array merge** (collides with any sibling tab's `enable`). New schema renames to `livechat_enabled` — fixes the collision risk.
- **NEW: First Pro tab where the new schema DROPS legacy fields entirely.** `wa_opening_method` + `wa_pre_filled_message` have no equivalents in `LiveChatSettingsSchema`. `Whatsapp.php:246`/`:267` will read `null` after migration (no fallback at the call site). WhatsApp pre-filled message disappears post-migration.
- **NEW: Second cross-module read coupling traced.** Elementor module reads `chat_button_seller_page` from LiveChat module (`StoreLiveChatButton.php:43,128`). Task 25 had Elementor↔RMA; this is Elementor↔LiveChat. Pattern: Elementor widgets/tags directly read sibling modules' options.
- **NEW: First Pro tab with non-trivial empty-default behavior.** Field `chat_button_seller_page` has no declared default in legacy schema, leaves `''` in the read row, and the helper `show_chat_on_store_page()` returns false via `dokan_validate_boolean('')`. New schema flips this to default `'on'` — semantic regression risk identical to Task 25 RMA refund/coupon flip.
- **NOT new:** sanitize_options no-op, MenuManager leak, unknown-key persistence, OVERWRITE save, plaintext storage of `<script>` / `javascript:` / serialized payloads, no `pre_update_option_*`/`update_option_*` listeners — all consistent with Tasks 12-25.

**API-key/token state in DB after task:** None — all sentinels prefixed `__T_FAKE_*` / `probe_*`. Final state: **option row restored** to the pre-trace 10-key snapshot (`FINAL_STATE=RESTORED`). The pre-trace row contained the empty-string `app_id=''` and `app_secret=''` (no real credentials), so no production secret was ever in play.

**Commit SHA:** `72844a11a` (self-referential; will be amended once at end of task chain if needed).

**Concerns:**
- **P0 — Plaintext credentials returned in GET response.** `app_id` + `app_secret` are returned unmasked. `secret_text: true` (legacy) and `variant: 'show_hide'` (new) are UI-only flags — the server-side response carries cleartext. Any actor with `manage_options` (admins, plus anyone with that cap via plugins) can fetch via the settings AJAX endpoint. **Mitigation:** wrap credential fields in a `response_sanitize_callback` that masks to `••••` + last 4 chars on read, or stop returning credential values entirely (only the existence flag).
- **P0 — Key renames in new schema with no migration code (6 keys).** `enable`, `provider`, `app_id`, `app_secret`, `chat_button_seller_page`, `chat_button_product_page` all rename. **13 read sites** reference legacy names. If wired-and-shipped without an upgrader, all 13 sites silently fall back to defaults. **Mitigation:** ship a one-shot upgrader that copies old→new keys in the live `dokan_live_chat` row, OR update read sites to alias both names, OR keep legacy names in the new schema.
- **P0 — Drop of `wa_opening_method` + `wa_pre_filled_message` with no migration.** `Whatsapp.php:246` + `:267` read these with **no default at the call site** — post-migration they return `null`/`''` and the WhatsApp chat button renders with empty opening behavior and empty pre-filled message. **Mitigation:** either keep these fields in the new schema, or add defaults at the read sites + drop the WhatsApp button entirely if intent is to remove WhatsApp support.
- **P0 — `provider='messenger'` re-introduction.** New schema's option list includes `messenger`, reversing a legacy deletion that had an explicit TODO comment at `AdminSettings.php:80` (`// TODO: Remove all the associated code for fb messenger`). Either the new schema's intent diverges from the legacy intent (now keeping messenger) or this is a copy-paste oversight. Decision point for the migration PR.
- **P1 — Default flip on `chat_button_seller_page`.** Empty default (legacy) → `'on'` default (new). Fresh installs show the chat button on every vendor store page by default. Semantic regression. Matches Task 25 RMA pattern.
- **P1 — Orphan `theme_color` read coupling.** `Messenger.php:39` echoes `theme_color` into HTML without it being a schema field. If a fresh install never has this key, the messenger render emits `theme_color=""`. **Mitigation:** either declare `theme_color` in the schema, or hardcode the legacy default `'#0084FF'` at the read site, or delete the messenger render path entirely (matching the TODO at `AdminSettings.php:80`).
- **P1 — Bare `enable` key.** Future flat-array merge will collide with any other tab's bare `enable`. New schema fixes via rename. **Mitigation:** ensure migration renames in DB, not just in schema.
- **P1 — `app_id` / `app_secret` have no value-format validation.** Empty strings persist. Garbage strings persist. No regex, no length check. A user can save a malformed TalkJS App ID and the schema accepts it; downstream `Talkjs.php` then fails to connect with no clear error trail.
- **P1 — Stored XSS surface on `wa_pre_filled_message`.** Free-form textarea, no `wp_kses_post`/`sanitize_textarea_field`. Stored XSS depends on the render site. `Whatsapp.php:267` builds a WhatsApp deep-link with this value — encoded into a URL, so XSS via the URL is mitigated by URL encoding, but reflected-into-DOM rendering elsewhere needs audit.
- **P2 — Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak persists.** Recurring.
- **P2 — Cross-module coupling.** `modules/elementor` reads `chat_button_seller_page`. If LiveChat module is disabled, the Elementor tag/widget still renders the wrapper but with no chat button — UI dead-end. Either gate the Elementor widget on LiveChat being active, or have the read fall back to hiding the wrapper.
- **P2 — `secret_text: true` flag is misleading.** Authors of the legacy schema likely believed `secret_text` triggered server-side redaction. It does not. Document this gap explicitly in the new-schema authoring guide.

### `dokan_wholesale` — Wholesale (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_wholesale`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_wholesale` returned `[]` (empty list / no persisted defaults). `wp option get dokan_wholesale` on the host install returned `Error: Could not get 'dokan_wholesale' option. Does it exist?` — confirming the option row **does not exist in `wp_options`** on this install (`STARTING_EXISTED=N` from the trace). **Notable:** wholesale is the **first Pro tab traced where the live install has no DB row at all** — every read site falls back to its hard-coded default at the call site. Captured `_trace_artifacts/dokan_wholesale_empty_default.json` (`db: []`, `response: []`).

**Source path:**
- Legacy schema: `dokan-pro/modules/wholesale/includes/clas-admin.php:36-87`. Class `\Dokan_Wholesale_Admin` (global namespace, no PSR-4), instantiated at `module.php:75` only when `is_admin()` (`new \Dokan_Wholesale_Admin();` inside `Module::initiate()`'s `if ( is_admin() )` block). **Same `is_admin()` gating gap as Task 25 RMA** — REST/cron/CLI saves would bypass the filter registration. Adds section `dokan_wholesale` via `dokan_settings_sections` filter at `:14` (priority 20), declares fields via `dokan_settings_fields` at `:15` (priority 20). **No `dokan_after_saving_settings` listener** on this module. **3 persisted fields:** `wholesale_price_display` (radio, default `'wholesale_customer'`, options `all_user|wholesale_customer`), `display_price_in_shop_archieve` (switcher, default `'on'`), `need_approval_for_wholesale_customer` (switcher, default `'on'`). **None of the 3 fields declares a `sanitize_callback` or `response_sanitize_callback`.** **No credential fields** in this tab. **Legacy spelling note:** `display_price_in_shop_archieve` ships with the misspelled key `archieve` (correct: `archive`) — it appears identically in the schema, in `class-cart-checkout.php:230`, and in the new schema's RENAME target — i.e., the typo is acknowledged-and-preserved everywhere but the new schema's variable name (which is itself a fresh key `wholesale_price_on_shop_archive`).
- **Current-branch schema (plugin-ui flat array):** `dokan-pro/modules/wholesale/includes/Schema/WholesaleSettingsSchema.php` — class **EXISTS but is NOT wired**. `grep -rn 'WholesaleSettingsSchema' dokan-pro/` returns only the class file itself; **no caller** instantiates or registers the schema. **Matches Task 25 RMA pattern** (defined-but-unwired), distinct from Task 26 live-chat (fully wired). Schema appends to `page_id: 'product'` as a `subpage` with `id: 'wholesale'`. **Renames all 3 keys + flips 2 defaults + renames the `all_user` option value:**
  - `wholesale_price_display` → `display_wholesale_pricing_to` (RENAME). Option-list value `all_user` → `all_users` (added trailing `s` — **silent value rename**). Variant `radio` → `radio_capsule`.
  - `display_price_in_shop_archieve` → `wholesale_price_on_shop_archive` (RENAME — also fixes the `archieve` typo in the key name). **Default flips: `'on'` → `'off'`.** Variant `switcher` → `switch`.
  - `need_approval_for_wholesale_customer` → `need_approval_for_customer` (RENAME, drops the `_wholesale_` qualifier; ambiguous outside the wholesale subpage context). **Default flips: `'on'` → `'off'`.** Variant `switcher` → `switch`.
  - **No DROPS, no ADDS** — same 3 logical fields, all 3 renamed.
- **Read sites (Pro-only, all reading `dokan_wholesale`):** 3 total, each reads a different field (1:1 mapping schema field → read site).
  - `dokan-pro/modules/wholesale/includes/functions.php:52` → `dokan_get_option('wholesale_price_display', 'dokan_wholesale', 'wholesale_customer')`. Consumed by `dokan_wholesale_can_see_price()` which gates **every wholesale price render path** (cart/checkout/loop/shop).
  - `dokan-pro/modules/wholesale/includes/class-cart-checkout.php:230` → `dokan_get_option('display_price_in_shop_archieve', 'dokan_wholesale', 'no')` — gates the shop-archive price-column render. **Read default is `'no'` but schema default is `'on'`** — first-time/missing-row reads get `'no'`, but fresh saves immediately flip to `'on'` (since the schema default applies on the first edit submit). **Default skew between schema and read site.**

No DB-only keys.
- **Save handler:** `dokan-lite/includes/Admin/Settings.php save_settings_value()`. AJAX action `dokan_save_settings`, nonce `dokan_admin`. OVERWRITE via `update_option('dokan_wholesale', $value)`. `sanitize_options($value, 'edit')` runs but **none of the 3 fields declares a `sanitize_callback`** — pass-through. Save flow fires `dokan_before_saving_settings` → `update_option` → `dokan_after_saving_settings`. **No module-level listener on either action for wholesale.**

Script `/tmp/trace_wholesale.php` drives `Settings::sanitize_options` via reflection + the same filter/action chains (`dokan_save_settings_value`, `dokan_before_saving_settings`, `dokan_after_saving_settings`, `dokan_get_settings_values`) used by the AJAX path. **Force-instantiated `Dokan_Wholesale_Admin`** to bypass the `is_admin()` gate in `module.php:74` (parity with Task 25 RMA methodology). Same code path semantics as AJAX; only HTTP transport differs.

**Boundary / filter findings:**
- **Status:** trace complete, round-trip PASS.
- **Field count:** 0 keys in initial DB (option row absent) → **17 keys after Save A** (3 schema + 13 probes + 1 MenuManager leak) → **4 keys after Save B** (3 schema + 1 MenuManager leak; OVERWRITE drops the 13 probes). OVERWRITE confirmed.
- **Save mode:** **OVERWRITE, fully unfiltered.** Response byte-identical to DB after `sanitize_options('read')` no-op pass and `dokan_get_settings_values` filter (no listener for this option).
- **Auth approach:** **`wp eval-file` fallback via host WP install** (`/Users/mahbub/Development/Projects/core-dokan`). Chrome session unauthenticated since Task 22; wp-env doesn't have Pro. Trace drives `Settings::sanitize_options` via reflection + the same filter/action chain the AJAX handler runs, and force-instantiates `Dokan_Wholesale_Admin` to bypass the `is_admin()` gate in `module.php:74` (same methodology as Task 25 RMA).
- **Filter findings:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Unchanged from Task 12 onward.
  - No `dokan_get_settings_values` transform on this option.
  - `sanitize_options` is a **NO-OP** for every wholesale field (no callbacks registered) — consistent with Tasks 22-26.
  - **No `dokan_after_saving_settings` listener** on this module. **No per-row actions.** **No side effects on save** (zero hooks fire beyond the generic Lite ones).
- **Empty-option default:** `[]` on read (after `delete_option`). **No self-seeder** writes defaults on first read. Initial DB state on this install was already `[]` (option row absent — `STARTING_EXISTED=N`). **First Pro tab traced where the live install has no DB row at all** (all prior Pro tabs had a pre-existing row, even if minimal).
- **Pro MenuManager leak:** present in both saves.
- **Unknown-key persistence:** `unknown_ws_field` + 12 boundary probes persisted in Save A, all dropped in Save B (OVERWRITE).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** No real credential fields in this tab, but the boundary probes `probe_sensitive_auth_token` / `probe_sensitive_secret_key` returned byte-identical in the response — confirms `sanitize_options` applies no key-name heuristics for masking (consistent with Task 26 finding).
- **Schema↔read-site key-skew audit:**
  - **Legacy schema (3 fields) vs 3 read sites:** **3/3 schema keys read by exactly one site each.** Minimum possible coupling. No DB-only keys. No orphans.
  - **Current-branch plugin-ui schema vs legacy:** `WholesaleSettingsSchema` is **defined but NOT wired** (no caller registers it). **Key skew if it ships as-is (after wiring):**
    - 3 RENAMES: `wholesale_price_display→display_wholesale_pricing_to`, `display_price_in_shop_archieve→wholesale_price_on_shop_archive` (typo fix), `need_approval_for_wholesale_customer→need_approval_for_customer`. **All 3 read sites still reference legacy names** — will silently fall back to call-site defaults after migration.
    - 1 silent option-value rename: `all_user` → `all_users` (added trailing `s`). Existing DB rows with `wholesale_price_display='all_user'` become "not in option list" under the new schema, but since `sanitize_options` doesn't enforce option lists, the value persists; the new UI radio just shows no selection.
    - 2 default flips: `display_price_in_shop_archieve` (`'on'` → `'off'`), `need_approval_for_wholesale_customer` (`'on'` → `'off'`). **Both flip away from the user-protective default** — shop archives stop showing wholesale prices by default, AND wholesale customer registrations auto-approve by default.
    - Typo persistence: legacy key `archieve` is read by `class-cart-checkout.php:230` and the new schema key is `archive`. Migration must remap both the option-key spelling and the read site (or alias both).
  - **DB-only keys (declared by neither schema):** `dashboard_menu_manager` (Pro MenuManager leak — recurring).
- **Cross-tab collision audit:**
  - **No DB collision** today (own `wp_options` row).
  - **No bare unprefixed keys** — all 3 legacy fields are domain-qualified (`wholesale_price_display`, `display_price_in_shop_archieve`, `need_approval_for_wholesale_customer`). Distinct from Task 26 LiveChat's bare `enable`/`provider`/`app_id`. **Safe under future flat-array merge.**
  - **Cross-plugin reads:** Lite does NOT read `dokan_wholesale`. **Cross-module reads: zero.** All 3 read sites live inside `modules/wholesale/`. **First Pro tab since Task 24 with no cross-module read coupling** (Tasks 25 RMA + 26 LiveChat both had Elementor cross-reads).
- **Does new `ProSettingsSchema` declare this tab?** **N — authored-but-unwired.** `WholesaleSettingsSchema.php` exists in `dokan-pro/modules/wholesale/includes/Schema/`, but **no caller** instantiates or registers it (`grep -rn 'WholesaleSettingsSchema' dokan-pro/` returns only the class file). **Matches Task 25 RMA pattern** (defined-but-unwired). Distinct from Task 26 LiveChat (fully wired) and from Task 24 (no class).
- **Sensitive-key wrapper observed?** **No.** No credential fields in this tab; probe confirms no key-name-based masking.
- **Round-trip:** `_trace_artifacts/dokan_wholesale_reload.json` byte-identical to `_trace_artifacts/dokan_wholesale_after_B.json` `.db` slice (`ROUND_TRIP=PASS`).
- **DB state restored:** trace script deleted the option row at end (since `STARTING_EXISTED=N`). `FINAL_STATE=RESTORED`. The live install's `dokan_wholesale` row remains absent — `dokan_get_option` reads fall back to call-site defaults exactly as before.

**Surprises:**
- **NEW: First Pro tab traced where the live install has no DB row at all.** `dokan_wholesale` option is absent. All read sites consume call-site defaults. (Tasks 1-26 all had some pre-existing row.)
- **NEW: First Pro tab traced where the new schema silently renames an enum value** (`all_user` → `all_users`). Pure cosmetic rename of an option-list value, no migration helper, would orphan any existing DB row's value. Trivial scope (one tab, one value) but a pattern to flag for the migration plan.
- **NEW: First Pro tab traced where the legacy schema ships a known typo in a key name** (`archieve`). The new schema's RENAME target uses the correct spelling (`archive`), which means the migration must remap both the spelling and the namespace. The typo is also baked into the read site `class-cart-checkout.php:230` — that line must change too (or alias both spellings).
- **NEW: First Pro tab traced with zero cross-module read coupling AND zero DB-only orphan keys.** Cleanest schema↔read-site fit of any Pro tab so far. (Tasks 25 RMA had Elementor↔RMA; Task 26 LiveChat had Elementor↔LiveChat + the `theme_color` orphan.)
- **NEW: Both switcher defaults flip away from the protective value in the new schema** (`'on'` → `'off'` for both). Wholesale prices stop appearing on shop archives by default; wholesale customer registrations auto-approve by default. Two simultaneous semantic regressions on a single tab — neither flagged by code review yet.
- **NOT new:** authored-but-unwired schema (Task 25 RMA); `is_admin()` gating gap on the legacy admin class (Task 25); 3/3 schema fields all renamed in the new schema (Task 26 had 6/8); sanitize_options no-op; MenuManager leak; unknown-key persistence; OVERWRITE save; plaintext storage of `<script>` / `javascript:` / serialized payloads; no `pre_update_option_*`/`update_option_*` listeners — all consistent with Tasks 12-26.

**API-key/token state in DB after task:** None — all sentinels prefixed `__T_FAKE_*` / `probe_*`. Final state: **option row absent** (matches pre-trace state — `FINAL_STATE=RESTORED`).

**Commit SHA:** `5b1a63f0d` (self-referential; may be amended at end of task chain).

**Concerns:**
- **P0 — Key renames in new schema with no migration code (3 keys).** `wholesale_price_display`, `display_price_in_shop_archieve`, `need_approval_for_wholesale_customer` all rename. **All 3 read sites** still reference legacy names. If wired-and-shipped without an upgrader, every read silently falls back to call-site defaults (which differ from schema defaults for `display_price_in_shop_archieve`: read site `'no'` vs schema `'off'`). **Mitigation:** ship a one-shot upgrader that copies old→new keys in any existing `dokan_wholesale` rows, OR update read sites to alias both names, OR keep legacy names in the new schema.
- **P0 — Schema is authored but unwired.** `WholesaleSettingsSchema.php` exists; no caller registers it on `dokan_get_admin_settings_schema`. Wholesale tab will be invisible in the new plugin-ui settings page. **Mitigation:** register inside `module.php::initiate()` (matching Task 26 LiveChat's `module.php:19` pattern: `( new WholesaleSettingsSchema() )->register()`).
- **P0 — Option-value rename `all_user` → `all_users` with no migration.** Existing DB rows with `'all_user'` become unselectable in the new UI radio (option list doesn't include the legacy value). Since `sanitize_options` doesn't enforce option lists, the legacy value persists in DB but appears as "no selection" in the UI; the read site `functions.php:52` continues to consume `'all_user'` verbatim. **Mitigation:** include `'all_user'` as a legacy alias in the new schema's options array, OR run a one-shot DB migration to rewrite all `'all_user'` → `'all_users'`, OR update the read site to alias both.
- **P1 — Two simultaneous default flips: shop archive + customer approval.** `display_price_in_shop_archieve` (`on→off`) and `need_approval_for_wholesale_customer` (`on→off`) both flip away from the user-protective default. Fresh installs on the new schema (a) stop showing wholesale prices on shop archives and (b) auto-approve every wholesale customer registration. **Mitigation:** keep legacy defaults, or document the flip in the migration changelog and surface a one-time admin notice.
- **P1 — Typo `archieve` in legacy key name with no migration helper.** `display_price_in_shop_archieve` is misspelled in schema + read site + DB. New schema's RENAME target uses the correct `archive`. Migration must update both the option key and the read site `class-cart-checkout.php:230`. **Mitigation:** include in the rename-aware upgrader; do NOT silently drop the legacy key.
- **P1 — `is_admin()` gating on `Dokan_Wholesale_Admin`.** Legacy admin class only registers its `dokan_settings_sections`/`dokan_settings_fields` filters when `is_admin()` returns true. REST/cron/CLI saves to `dokan_wholesale` would bypass the filter registration. Identical to Task 25 RMA gap; same mitigation applies (register the filter handlers unconditionally; gate only the metabox handlers on `is_admin()`).
- **P1 — Read-site default skew on `display_price_in_shop_archieve`.** Read site uses `'no'` as third-arg default; legacy schema declares `'on'`; new schema declares `'off'`. Three different "default" answers depending on which code path runs first. **Mitigation:** unify on a single default; prefer the schema authority.
- **P2 — Pro MenuManager `dashboard_menu_manager: []` leak persists.** Recurring across all 26 prior Pro tabs.
- **P2 — `sanitize_options` does not enforce option lists.** Any `radio`/`select` field accepts any string value, including values not in the declared `options` array (confirmed in Save A with the probe). Low impact for wholesale (only `wholesale_price_display` is a radio), but pattern affects every Pro tab.

### `dokan_germanized` — EU Compliance Fields (Pro)

**wp_option name:** `dokan_germanized`

**Initial GET slice (pre-trace):** `/tmp/dokan_initial_get.json → .data.dokan_germanized` returned a fully-populated row with 6 keys (`vendor_fields`, `vendor_registration`, `customer_fields`, `enabled_germanized`, `override_invoice_number`, `dashboard_menu_manager`). `wp option get dokan_germanized` on the host install returned the same row — `STARTING_EXISTED=Y`. Captured `_trace_artifacts/dokan_germanized_baseline_db.json`. Empty-default probe captured at `_trace_artifacts/dokan_germanized_empty_default.json` (`db: []`, `response: []`) — confirming **no self-seeder** on this option.

**Source path:**
- Legacy schema: `dokan-pro/modules/germanized/includes/Admin/Settings.php:13-120`. Class `\WeDevs\DokanPro\Modules\Germanized\Admin\Settings` (PSR-4). Adds section `dokan_germanized` via `dokan_settings_sections` filter at `:21` (priority 20), declares fields via `dokan_settings_fields` at `:22` (priority 20). Constructor registers both filters unconditionally — **no `is_admin()` gate at the class level** (distinct from Task 25 RMA and Task 27 Wholesale). The module's `module.php` instantiates this admin class — need to verify whether under `is_admin()`. Trace drives `Settings::sanitize_options` via reflection + the same filter/action chain the AJAX handler runs. **3-arg signature for `dokan_before_saving_settings` / `dokan_after_saving_settings`** is required (a global TableRate `Notices::clear_notice_cache` listener fatals with 2 args) — captured in the script and reusable for remaining tabs. Force-instantiated `WeDevs\DokanPro\Modules\Germanized\Admin\Settings` as a safety even though its constructor is not `is_admin()`-gated.
- **Filter findings:**
  - `dokan_save_settings_value` p10 — Pro MenuManager appends `dashboard_menu_manager: []`. Unchanged from Task 12 onward.

  - **Current-branch plugin-ui schema vs legacy:** `GermanizedSettingsSchema` is **defined AND wired** at `module.php:36` (matches Task 26 LiveChat's wiring pattern; distinct from Tasks 25/27's authored-but-unwired). **Key skew if it ships as-is:**
    - 5 RENAMES of the top-level keys: `vendor_fields→vendor_extra_fields`, `vendor_registration→eu_vendor_registration_display`, `customer_fields→customer_extra_fields`, `enabled_germanized→germanized_support_vendors`, `override_invoice_number→vendor_invoice_number_override`. **All 6 read sites still reference legacy names** — will silently fall back to call-site defaults after migration.
    - 9 value-string renames inside the two multicheck option lists (5 in `vendor_fields`, 4 in `customer_fields`) — **zero shared identifiers** between legacy and new value strings (other than `vat_number` substring overlap which is itself a rename).
    - 2 default flips on the multichecks: `vendor_fields` (all 5 → `['company_name']`), `customer_fields` (all 4 → `['bank_iban']`). **Both flip toward emptier defaults** — fresh installs render almost no EU compliance fields by default; the read sites `Helper::get_seller_fields()` and `Helper::get_customer_fields()` decide what to render based on this array.
    - **Semantic concern:** the `billing_` prefix on `customer_fields` legacy values is load-bearing — it's the literal `<input name="...">` attribute emitted by `CustomFields/Billing.php`. Dropping the prefix in the new option-list values would break the read-render mapping.

- **Cross-tab collision audit:**
  - **No DB collision** today (own `wp_options` row).
  - **Some bare unprefixed keys** — `vendor_fields`, `customer_fields`, `vendor_registration` are not domain-qualified (no `germanized_` / `eu_` prefix). Other modules could plausibly declare a `vendor_fields` or `customer_fields` setting too. The new schema fixes this by renaming all 5 to qualified forms (`eu_*` / `germanized_*` / `vendor_extra_*` / `customer_extra_*`).

The new schema appends to Lite's `compliance` page as a `subpage` (not a top-level page), which is **a different structural pattern** from prior Pro tabs that registered their own top-level pages.
